<file path="src/components/ApparelModal.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Shirt, Download, ExternalLink, ShieldAlert, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';
import { renderTourBackGraphic } from '../services/tourCanvasEngine';
import { renderHighResFrontGraphic } from '../services/apparelCanvasEngine';

interface ApparelModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  album: AlbumEntry | null;
  onNotify?: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info', text: string) => void;
}

const INK_PALETTES = [
  { label: 'Crimson Red', hex: '#8b1410' },
  { label: 'Amber Gold', hex: '#d97706' },
  { label: 'Warm Eggshell', hex: '#f3ede4' },
];

const FABRICATORS = [
  { name: 'CustomInk', url: 'https://www.customink.com/lab' },
  { name: 'Zazzle', url: 'https://www.zazzle.com/custom/tshirts' },
  { name: 'Spreadshirt', url: 'https://www.spreadshirt.com/create-your-own' },
];

export const ApparelModal: React.FC<ApparelModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  album,
  onNotify,
}) => {
  const [frontMode, setFrontMode] = useState<'cutout' | 'plaque'>('cutout');
  const [includeBack, setIncludeBack] = useState(true);
  const [inkColor, setInkColor] = useState('#8b1410');
  const [frontDataUrl, setFrontDataUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [backDataUrl, setBackDataUrl] = useState<string>('');
  const [isRendering, setIsRendering] = useState(false);

  // Render high-res previews whenever options change
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen || !album) return;
    setIsRendering(true);

    renderHighResFrontGraphic(album, frontMode).then((fUrl) => {
      setFrontDataUrl(fUrl);
      const bUrl = renderTourBackGraphic({ accentColor: inkColor });
      setBackDataUrl(bUrl);
      setIsRendering(false);
    });
  }, [isOpen, album, frontMode, inkColor]);

  if (!isOpen || !album) return null;

  const triggerDownload = (url: string, filename: string) => {
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
  };

  const handleExport = (destinationUrl?: string) => {
    const slug = album.bandName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '_');

    // 1. Download front chest print
    triggerDownload(frontDataUrl, `${slug}_front_300dpi.png`);

    // 2. Download tour back print if enabled
    if (includeBack) {
      setTimeout(() => {
        triggerDownload(backDataUrl, `${slug}_back_tour_300dpi.png`);
      }, 350);
    }

    if (onNotify) {
      onNotify('success', 'Master 300 DPI production files dispatched to downloads.');
    }

    // 3. Launch third-party vendor portal if selected
    if (destinationUrl) {
      window.open(destinationUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-3 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-[#161310] border border-amber-800/40 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[92vh]">
        {/* Terminal Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#12100e] shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Shirt className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-display tracking-wider text-base text-[#f3ede4] uppercase">
                Apparel Fabrication Terminal
              </h3>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-400/80 font-mono block -mt-0.5">
                300 DPI Direct-To-Garment Production Handoff
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/80 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Configuration Body */}
        <div className="p-4 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto text-xs text-slate-300 flex-1">
          {/* Side-by-Side Dual Previews */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            {/* Front Panel Preview */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block font-bold">
                Front: Chest Plate
              </span>
              <div className="aspect-square rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-black/70 p-2 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                {isRendering || !frontDataUrl ? (
                  <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-amber-500 animate-pulse">Rendering...</span>
                ) : (
                  <img src={frontDataUrl} alt="Front print" className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain" />
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Back Panel Preview */}
            <div className="space-y-1.5 text-center">
              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block font-bold">
                Back: Tour Roster
              </span>
              <div className={`aspect-square rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-black/70 p-2 flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden ${!includeBack ? 'opacity-30' : ''}`}>
                {!includeBack ? (
                  <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-slate-500">Back Disabled</span>
                ) : isRendering || !backDataUrl ? (
                  <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-amber-500 animate-pulse">Rendering...</span>
                ) : (
                  <img src={backDataUrl} alt="Back tour print" className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain" />
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Front Mode Toggles */}
          <div className="space-y-1.5 bg-[#100e0c] p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800">
            <label className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 font-bold block">
              Front Print Format
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrontMode('cutout')}
                className={`p-2 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${
                  frontMode === 'cutout'
                    ? 'border-amber-500/60 bg-amber-500/10 text-white'
                    : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="font-bold text-xs block">Isolated Cutout</span>
                <span className="text-[9.5px] leading-tight block text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                  Transparent alpha; fabric shows through (ideal for black shirts).
                </span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrontMode('plaque')}
                className={`p-2 rounded-lg border text-left transition-all ${
                  frontMode === 'plaque'
                    ? 'border-amber-500/60 bg-amber-500/10 text-white'
                    : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 text-slate-400'
                }`}
              >
                <span className="font-bold text-xs block">Full Sleeve Plaque</span>
                <span className="text-[9.5px] leading-tight block text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                  Solid 1:1 square sleeve art with paper distress &amp; backdrop.
                </span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Back Tour Dates & Color Toggles */}
          <div className="space-y-2 bg-[#100e0c] p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <label className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Tour Schedule (Back Print)</span>
              </label>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setIncludeBack(!includeBack)}
                className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono font-bold uppercase transition-all border ${
                  includeBack
                    ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/40'
                    : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 border-slate-700'
                }`}
              >
                {includeBack ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
              </button>
            </div>

            {includeBack && (
              <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-800/80 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">Back Ink Pigment:</span>
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  {INK_PALETTES.map((pal) => (
                    <button
                      key={pal.hex}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setInkColor(pal.hex)}
                      className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-mono border transition-all ${
                        inkColor === pal.hex
                          ? 'border-amber-400 bg-slate-800 text-white'
                          : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 text-slate-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full" style={{ backgroundColor: pal.hex }} />
                      <span>{pal.label}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Institutional Separation Disclaimer */}
          <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-950/20 border border-amber-800/40 space-y-1.5 text-amber-200/90 font-mono">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-400 font-bold text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
              <span>Notice: No Affiliation With Vendors</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-[9.5px] leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
              Once you are redirected to the vendor&apos;s site, you are on your own! All sizing and color choices happen independently, as Y.D.R.B.N. has no commercial ties and assumes zero liability for clothing items, orders, or deliveries. Y.D.R.B.N. does not recommend any particular vendors, these were just handy options. DO NOT FOLD, SPINDLE, OR MUTILATE Y.D.R.B.N.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer Dispatch Actions */}
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#12100e] space-y-2 shrink-0">
          <span className="text-[9.5px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 block text-center">
            Dispatch Master 300 DPI Assets To Fabricator:
          </span>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            {FABRICATORS.map((fab) => (
              <button
                key={fab.name}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleExport(fab.url)}
                className="py-1.5 px-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 hover:border-amber-500/50 text-[#f3ede4] text-[11px] font-mono font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm"
              >
                <span>{fab.name}</span>
                <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => handleExport()}
            className="w-full py-2 rounded-lg bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-amber-600 hover:from-amber-400 hover:to-amber-500 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider font-mono flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-md active:scale-95 transition-all"
          >
            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Download Master 300 DPI PNGs Only</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/ArchiveDrawer.tsx">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';
import { X, Heart, Trash2, Disc, Search, Music, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';

interface ArchiveDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  albums: AlbumEntry[];
  currentId?: string;
  onSelectAlbum: (album: AlbumEntry) => void;
  onToggleFavorite: (id: string) => void;
  onDeleteAlbum: (id: string) => void;
}

export const ArchiveDrawer: React.FC<ArchiveDrawerProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  albums,
  currentId,
  onSelectAlbum,
  onToggleFavorite,
  onDeleteAlbum,
}) => {
  const [filterFavorites, setFilterFavorites] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const filteredAlbums = albums.filter((album) => {
    if (filterFavorites && !album.isFavorite) return false;
    if (searchQuery.trim()) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      const matchBand = album.bandName.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchTitle = album.albumTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchCatalog = album.catalogNumber.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      return matchBand || matchTitle || matchCatalog;
    }
    return true;
  });

  const favoritesCount = albums.filter((a) => a.isFavorite).length;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex justify-end bg-black/80 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm h-full bg-[#101422] border-l border-slate-800 flex flex-col shadow-2xl animate-in slide-in-from-right duration-200">
        {/* Drawer Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0c101d]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Disc className="w-4 h-4 text-orange-400" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-sm tracking-wide text-white">
                Vinyl Crate Archive
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono">
                {albums.length} Pressings Preserved
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close archive"
            className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Filter & Search Bar */}
        <div className="p-3 border-b border-slate-800/80 bg-slate-900/60 space-y-2">
          {/* Search input */}
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-500" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search band, title, catalog..."
              className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-800/80 border border-slate-700 text-xs text-slate-100 placeholder-slate-500 focus:outline-none focus:border-orange-500"
            />
            {searchQuery && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSearchQuery('')}
                className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
              >
                <X className="w-3 h-3" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Filter Pills */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <button
              onClick={() => setFilterFavorites(false)}
              className={`flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-all border ${
                !filterFavorites
                  ? 'bg-orange-500/20 text-orange-300 border-orange-500/40 shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-slate-800/50 text-slate-400 border-slate-700/60 hover:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              All Records ({albums.length})
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setFilterFavorites(true)}
              className={`flex-1 py-1 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1 transition-all border ${
                filterFavorites
                  ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border-rose-500/40 shadow-sm'
                  : 'bg-slate-800/50 text-slate-400 border-slate-700/60 hover:text-slate-200'
              }`}
            >
              <Heart className="w-3 h-3 fill-rose-500 text-rose-500" />
              Favorites ({favoritesCount})
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Record List */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-3 space-y-2 divide-y divide-slate-800/40">
          {filteredAlbums.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center h-48 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs p-4">
              <Disc className="w-8 h-8 text-slate-600 mb-2 stroke-1" />
              <p>No records found in this crate.</p>
              {filterFavorites && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => setFilterFavorites(false)}
                  className="mt-2 text-orange-400 hover:underline text-[11px]"
                >
                  View all records
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            filteredAlbums.map((album) => {
              const isSelected = album.id === currentId;
              return (
                <div
                  key={album.id}
                  className={`pt-2 first:pt-0 flex items-center gap-2.5 p-2 rounded-xl transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-500/30'
                      : 'hover:bg-slate-800/50'
                  }`}
                >
                  {/* Thumbnail */}
                  <div
                    onClick={() => {
                      onSelectAlbum(album);
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className="relative w-12 h-12 rounded-md overflow-hidden bg-slate-800 shrink-0 cursor-pointer shadow-md group"
                  >
                    {album.coverImageUrl ? (
                      <img
                        src={album.coverImageUrl}
                        alt={album.albumTitle}
                        className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-slate-800 text-slate-500">
                        <Disc className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" />
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {isSelected && (
                      <div className="absolute inset-0 border-2 border-orange-500 rounded-md pointer-events-none" />
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Album Info */}
                  <div
                    onClick={() => {
                      onSelectAlbum(album);
                      onClose();
                    }}
                    className="flex-1 min-w-0 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-100 truncate hover:text-orange-400">
                      {album.bandName}
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200/80 italic truncate">
                      {album.albumTitle}
                    </p>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono mt-0.5">
                      <span>{album.year}</span>
                      <span>&bull;</span>
                      <span className="truncate">{album.catalogNumber}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Actions */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
                    <button
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        onToggleFavorite(album.id);
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-400 transition-all active:scale-90"
                      title={album.isFavorite ? 'Remove favorite' : 'Add favorite'}
                      aria-label="Toggle favorite"
                    >
                      <Heart
                        className={`w-4 h-4 ${
                          album.isFavorite ? 'fill-rose-500 text-rose-500' : ''
                        }`}
                      />
                    </button>

                    {albums.length > 1 && (
                      <button
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (
                            window.confirm(
                              `Discard "${album.bandName}" from your archive?`
                            )
                          ) {
                            onDeleteAlbum(album.id);
                          }
                        }}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-400 transition-all active:scale-90"
                        title="Delete from archive"
                        aria-label="Delete album"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/CrateForgeModal.tsx">
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Hammer,
  Upload,
  Download,
  BookOpen,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Trash2,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  FileJson,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface CustomPackManifest {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  version: string;
  author: string;
  isCustom: true;
  createdAt: number;
}

export interface CustomPackWords {
  nouns: string[];
  adjectives: string[];
  ensembles: string[];
}

export interface CustomStamp {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  dataUrl: string;
}

export interface CustomPackBundle {
  manifest: CustomPackManifest;
  words: CustomPackWords;
  stamps?: CustomStamp[];
}

interface CrateForgeModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSavePack: (bundle: CustomPackBundle) => Promise<void> | void;
  onNotify?: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info', text: string) => void;
  initialPack?: CustomPackBundle | null;
}

type ForgeTab = 'vocabulary' | 'cutouts';

export const CrateForgeModal: React.FC<CrateForgeModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSavePack,
  onNotify,
  initialPack,
}) => {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ForgeTab>('vocabulary');

  // Metadata State
  const [packName, setPackName] = useState(initialPack?.manifest.name || '');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initialPack?.manifest.description || '');
  const [author, setAuthor] = useState(initialPack?.manifest.author || 'Anonymous Archivist');
  const [version, setVersion] = useState(initialPack?.manifest.version || '1.0.0');

  // Raw Word Textarea Inputs
  const [rawNouns, setRawNouns] = useState(initialPack?.words.nouns.join('\n') || '');
  const [rawAdjectives, setRawAdjectives] = useState(initialPack?.words.adjectives.join('\n') || '');
  const [rawEnsembles, setRawEnsembles] = useState(initialPack?.words.ensembles.join('\n') || '');

  // Subject Cutout Stamps
  const [stamps, setStamps] = useState<CustomStamp[]>(initialPack?.stamps || []);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
  const [validationError, setValidationError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const jsonImportRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  // Helper: Parses comma- or newline-delimited strings into clean deduplicated lists
  const parseWordString = (raw: string): string[] => {
    return Array.from(
      new Set(
        raw
          .split(/[\n,]/)
          .map((w) => w.trim())
          .filter((w) => w.length > 0)
      )
    );
  };

  const parsedNouns = parseWordString(rawNouns);
  const parsedAdjectives = parseWordString(rawAdjectives);
  const parsedEnsembles = parseWordString(rawEnsembles);

  // Helper: Generates a contextual name if the user leaves the input blank
  const resolveCrateName = (): string => {
    if (packName.trim()) return packName.trim();

    if (stamps.length > 0) {
      const cleanStem = stamps[0].name.replace(/[-_]+/g, ' ').trim();
      if (cleanStem) {
        return `${cleanStem.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + cleanStem.slice(1)} Crate`;
      }
      return `Visual Stamp Crate #${Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900)}`;
    }

    if (parsedNouns.length > 0) {
      return `${parsedNouns[0]} Crate`;
    }

    if (parsedAdjectives.length > 0) {
      return `${parsedAdjectives[0]} Crate`;
    }

    return `Custom Crate #${Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900)}`;
  };

  // Handle Image Cutout Uploads (PNG / WebP transparent subjects)
  const handleImageUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const files = e.target.files;
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

    Array.from(files).forEach((file) => {
      if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
        if (onNotify) onNotify('error', `${file.name} is not an image.`);
        return;
      }

      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = () => {
        const dataUrl = reader.result as string;
        const newStamp: CustomStamp = {
          id: `stamp-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 6)}`,
          name: file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ''),
          dataUrl,
        };
        setStamps((prev) => [...prev, newStamp]);
      };
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    });

    if (e.target) e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleRemoveStamp = (id: string) => {
    setStamps((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== id));
  };

  // Export current pack directly as a portable .json bundle
  const handleExportJson = () => {
    const finalName = resolveCrateName();

    const bundle: CustomPackBundle = {
      manifest: {
        id: initialPack?.manifest.id || `pack-user-${Date.now()}`,
        name: finalName,
        description: description.trim() || 'A custom YDRBN crate.',
        version: version.trim() || '1.0.0',
        author: author.trim() || 'Archivist',
        isCustom: true,
        createdAt: initialPack?.manifest.createdAt || Date.now(),
      },
      words: {
        nouns: parsedNouns,
        adjectives: parsedAdjectives,
        ensembles: parsedEnsembles,
      },
      stamps,
    };

    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(bundle, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = `${finalName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '_')}-crate.json`;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    if (onNotify) onNotify('success', `Pack "${finalName}" exported to JSON bundle.`);
  };

  // Import an external .json pack file directly into the forge
  const handleImportJson = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      try {
        const bundle = JSON.parse(event.target?.result as string) as CustomPackBundle;
        if (!bundle.manifest?.name || !bundle.words) {
          throw new Error('Invalid pack schema. Missing manifest or words.');
        }

        setPackName(bundle.manifest.name);
        setDescription(bundle.manifest.description || '');
        setAuthor(bundle.manifest.author || 'Archivist');
        setVersion(bundle.manifest.version || '1.0.0');

        setRawNouns((bundle.words.nouns || []).join('\n'));
        setRawAdjectives((bundle.words.adjectives || []).join('\n'));
        setRawEnsembles((bundle.words.ensembles || []).join('\n'));
        setStamps(bundle.stamps || []);

        setValidationError(null);
        if (onNotify) onNotify('success', `Imported "${bundle.manifest.name}" crate!`);
      } catch (err: any) {
        setValidationError(`Import failed: ${err.message}`);
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    if (e.target) e.target.value = '';
  };

  // Assemble and save the pack into the app
  const handleSave = async () => {
    setValidationError(null);

    if (parsedNouns.length === 0 && parsedAdjectives.length === 0 && stamps.length === 0) {
      setValidationError('Provide at least one noun, adjective, or cutout stamp.');
      return;
    }

    const finalName = resolveCrateName();

    setIsSaving(true);
    try {
      const bundle: CustomPackBundle = {
        manifest: {
          id: initialPack?.manifest.id || `pack-user-${Date.now()}`,
          name: finalName,
          description: description.trim() || 'Custom user crate.',
          version: version.trim() || '1.0.0',
          author: author.trim() || 'Archivist',
          isCustom: true,
          createdAt: initialPack?.manifest.createdAt || Date.now(),
        },
        words: {
          nouns: parsedNouns,
          adjectives: parsedAdjectives,
          ensembles: parsedEnsembles,
        },
        stamps,
      };

      await onSavePack(bundle);
      if (onNotify) onNotify('success', `Mounted "${finalName}" into Cargo Bay!`);
      onClose();
    } catch (err: any) {
      setValidationError(err.message || 'Failed to assemble custom pack.');
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-3 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-[#111625] border border-amber-500/50 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[92vh]">
        {/* Modal Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0c101d] shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Hammer className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-display tracking-wider text-base text-white uppercase">
                The Crate Forge
              </h3>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-400/80 font-mono block -mt-0.5">
                Offline Modular Content Studio
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close Crate Forge"
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/80 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Persistent Crate Identity Header (Always visible across all tabs) */}
        <div className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-[#161310] border-b border-slate-800/80 flex items-center gap-2.5 shrink-0">
          <label className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-400 font-bold shrink-0">
            Crate Name:
          </label>
          <input
            type="text"
            value={packName}
            onChange={(e) => setPackName(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="e.g. Victorian Eccentrics, Basement Tools (optional)..."
            className="flex-1 bg-black/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-100 placeholder:text-slate-600 focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500 font-mono"
          />
        </div>

        {/* Tab Selection Bar */}
        <div className="flex border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0e1320] shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('vocabulary')}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all border-b-2 ${
              activeTab === 'vocabulary'
                ? 'border-amber-400 text-amber-300 bg-amber-500/10'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <BookOpen className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Words &amp; Metadata</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-300">
              {parsedNouns.length + parsedAdjectives.length + parsedEnsembles.length}
            </span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('cutouts')}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all border-b-2 ${
              activeTab === 'cutouts'
                ? 'border-amber-400 text-amber-300 bg-amber-500/10'
                : 'border-transparent text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200'
            }`}
          >
            <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Subject Stamps</span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-300">
              {stamps.length}
            </span>
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Modal Content */}
        <div className="p-4 space-y-3.5 overflow-y-auto text-xs text-slate-300 flex-1">
          {validationError && (
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-950/40 border border-rose-800/60 text-rose-300 text-[11px] flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-rose-400" />
              <span>{validationError}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'vocabulary' && (
            <>
              {/* Secondary Pack Metadata */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Archivist / Author
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={author}
                    onChange={(e) => setAuthor(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Author name"
                    className="w-full p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500 font-mono"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Version
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={version}
                    onChange={(e) => setVersion(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="1.0.0"
                    className="w-full p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500 font-mono"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="col-span-2 space-y-1">
                  <label className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Description / Lore
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={description}
                    onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="A brief note on the aesthetic of this pack..."
                    className="w-full p-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="border-t border-slate-800/80 my-1" />

              {/* Nouns Input */}
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-amber-300 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Nouns (One per line or comma-separated)
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
                    {parsedNouns.length} loaded
                  </span>
                </div>
                <textarea
                  value={rawNouns}
                  onChange={(e) => setRawNouns(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Spork, Radiator, Bureaucrat, Flannel, Lawn Chair..."
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-amber-500 leading-relaxed font-mono resize-none placeholder:text-slate-600"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Adjectives Input */}
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-emerald-300 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Adjectives (One per line or comma-separated)
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
                    {parsedAdjectives.length} loaded
                  </span>
                </div>
                <textarea
                  value={rawAdjectives}
                  onChange={(e) => setRawAdjectives(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Overpriced, Melancholic, Resonant, Beige, Unironic..."
                  rows={3}
                  className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 leading-relaxed font-mono resize-none placeholder:text-slate-600"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Ensembles / Formats Input */}
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-sky-300 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Ensembles / Group Formats (Optional)
                  </label>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
                    {parsedEnsembles.length} loaded
                  </span>
                </div>
                <textarea
                  value={rawEnsembles}
                  onChange={(e) => setRawEnsembles(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Quartet, Collective, Society, Department, Experience..."
                  rows={2}
                  className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-sky-500 leading-relaxed font-mono resize-none placeholder:text-slate-600"
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {activeTab === 'cutouts' && (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-300">
                  <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span className="font-bold text-xs">Transparent PNG Subject Stamps</span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                  Upload cutouts (transparent PNG or WebP) to appear as surreal album centerpieces. Images are converted and stored locally in your browser&apos;s IndexedDB.
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Dropzone / Upload Action */}
              <div
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                className="p-4 border-2 border-dashed border-slate-700 hover:border-amber-400 rounded-xl bg-slate-950/60 flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer transition-colors group"
              >
                <Upload className="w-6 h-6 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-amber-400 transition-colors" />
                <div className="text-center">
                  <span className="font-bold text-slate-300 block text-xs group-hover:text-white">
                    Select PNG / WebP Cutouts
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">
                    Transparent backgrounds recommended
                  </span>
                </div>
                <input
                  ref={fileInputRef}
                  type="file"
                  multiple
                  accept="image/png,image/webp"
                  onChange={handleImageUpload}
                  className="hidden"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Gallery of Added Stamps */}
              {stamps.length > 0 ? (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 pt-1">
                  {stamps.map((stamp) => (
                    <div
                      key={stamp.id}
                      className="group relative rounded-lg border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950 p-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-between overflow-hidden"
                    >
                      <div className="w-full h-16 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 rounded p-1">
                        <img
                          src={stamp.dataUrl}
                          alt={stamp.name}
                          className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-400 truncate w-full text-center mt-1 px-1">
                        {stamp.name}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleRemoveStamp(stamp.id)}
                        className="absolute top-1 right-1 p-1 bg-rose-950/80 hover:bg-rose-900 text-rose-300 rounded opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
                        aria-label="Remove stamp"
                      >
                        <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="py-6 text-center text-slate-500 text-xs italic">
                  No cutout stamps added to this crate yet.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer Actions */}
        <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#0c101d] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {/* Import JSON */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => jsonImportRef.current?.click()}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95"
              title="Import pack from JSON file"
            >
              <FileJson className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
              <span>Import</span>
            </button>
            <input
              ref={jsonImportRef}
              type="file"
              accept=".json,application/json"
              onChange={handleImportJson}
              className="hidden"
            />

            {/* Export JSON */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleExportJson}
              className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border border-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95"
              title="Download pack as JSON"
            >
              <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-sky-400" />
              <span>Export</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-colors"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSave}
              disabled={isSaving}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-slate-950 bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-300 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-600 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-md active:scale-95"
            >
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{isSaving ? 'Assembling...' : 'Assemble Crate'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/CustomPhotoControls.tsx">
import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import { ImagePlus, Sliders, Check, X } from 'lucide-react';

interface CustomPhotoControlsProps {
  useCustomPhotos: boolean;
  onToggleUseCustomPhotos: (val: boolean) => void;
  selectedPhoto: string | null;
  onSelectPhoto: (base64: string | null) => void;
  onModifyPhoto: () => void;
  onUsePhotoAsIs: () => void;
  isLoading: boolean;
}

export const CustomPhotoControls: React.FC<CustomPhotoControlsProps> = ({
  useCustomPhotos,
  onToggleUseCustomPhotos,
  selectedPhoto,
  onSelectPhoto,
  onModifyPhoto,
  onUsePhotoAsIs,
  isLoading,
}) => {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      const result = reader.result as string;
      onSelectPhoto(result);
      onToggleUseCustomPhotos(true);
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full px-3 py-1 space-y-1.5 z-20">
      <input
        ref={fileInputRef}
        type="file"
        accept="image/*"
        onChange={handleFileChange}
        className="hidden"
      />

      {/* Row 1: [ ] "Use custom photos for cover generation" checkbox aligned horizontally next to a [Select Photos] button */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 bg-slate-900/70 border border-slate-800/80 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 shadow-sm">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer select-none min-w-0">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={useCustomPhotos}
            onChange={(e) => onToggleUseCustomPhotos(e.target.checked)}
            className="w-4 h-4 rounded text-orange-500 bg-slate-800 border-slate-700 focus:ring-orange-500 focus:ring-offset-0 cursor-pointer accent-orange-500"
          />
          <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-slate-300 truncate">
            Use custom photos for cover generation
          </span>
        </label>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
          {selectedPhoto && (
            <div className="relative w-6 h-6 rounded overflow-hidden border border-orange-400">
              <img src={selectedPhoto} alt="Selected" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onSelectPhoto(null)}
                className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center opacity-0 hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"
                title="Remove photo"
              >
                <X className="w-3 h-3 text-white" />
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold px-2 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <ImagePlus className="w-3 h-3 text-orange-400" />
            <span>Select Photos</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Row 2: Two centered action buttons: [Modify Your Photos] and [Use Your Photos (as is)] */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onModifyPhoto}
          disabled={!selectedPhoto || isLoading}
          title="Applies authentic 1970s Kodachrome film grain & ring-wear sheen directly on canvas"
          className="flex-1 max-w-[170px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold py-1 px-2 rounded-full bg-slate-800/90 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 hover:bg-slate-700 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm"
        >
          <Sliders className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
          <span className="truncate">Modify Your Photos</span>
        </button>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onUsePhotoAsIs}
          disabled={!selectedPhoto || isLoading}
          className="flex-1 max-w-[170px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-semibold py-1 px-2 rounded-full bg-slate-800/90 text-slate-200 border border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-700 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-40 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-sm"
        >
          <Check className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-400" />
          <span className="truncate">Use Your Photos (as is)</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

<file path="src/components/DailyDropButton.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import { Zap, Clock, Disc } from 'lucide-react';
import { formatCooldown } from '../services/storageService';

interface DailyDropButtonProps {
  onDailyDrop: () => void;
  isLoading: boolean;
  cooldownMs: number;
  isDevMode: boolean;
}

export const DailyDropButton: React.FC<DailyDropButtonProps> = ({
  onDailyDrop,
  isLoading,
  cooldownMs,
  isDevMode,
}) => {
  const isCooldownActive = cooldownMs > 0 && !isDevMode;

  return (
    <div className="w-full px-3 py-1">
      <button
        onClick={onDailyDrop}
        disabled={isLoading || isCooldownActive}
        className={`w-full h-9 px-3 rounded-lg font-black text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-wider uppercase transition-all shadow-lg active:scale-[0.98] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 border ${
          isLoading
            ? 'bg-orange-700/60 text-orange-200 border-orange-500/50 cursor-wait'
            : isCooldownActive
            ? 'bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-400 border-slate-700/80 cursor-not-allowed opacity-80'
            : 'bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 via-amber-500 to-orange-600 hover:from-orange-400 hover:via-amber-400 hover:to-orange-500 text-slate-950 border-amber-300/40 shadow-orange-500/25 animate-pulse'
        }`}
      >
        {isLoading ? (
          <>
            <Disc className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin text-orange-200 shrink-0" />
            <span>PRESSING VINYL...</span>
          </>
        ) : isCooldownActive ? (
          <>
            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
            <span>DAILY DROP IN {formatCooldown(cooldownMs)}</span>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Zap className="w-3.5 h-3.5 fill-current text-slate-950 shrink-0" />
            <span>
              ⚡ DAILY DROP {isDevMode ? '(DEV UNLOCKED)' : ''}
            </span>
          </>
        )}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/DispatchModal.tsx">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Radio, Send, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { transmitDispatch, getAnonymousDeviceId } from '../services/feedbackService';

interface DispatchModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onNotify?: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'info', text: string) => void;
}

const CATEGORIES = [
  'Expansion Pack Theme',
  'Vocabulary / Word Submissions',
  'Visual Gag / Cutout Idea',
  'System Anomaly / Bug',
] as const;

export const DispatchModal: React.FC<DispatchModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose, onNotify }) => {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<string>(CATEGORIES[0]);
  const [content, setContent] = useState('');
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [isSuccess, setIsSuccess] = useState(false);
  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const deviceId = getAnonymousDeviceId();
  const shortId = deviceId.substring(0, 8);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErrorMsg(null);
    setIsSubmitting(true);

    const result = await transmitDispatch(category, content);
    setIsSubmitting(false);

    if (result.success) {
      setIsSuccess(true);
      if (onNotify) onNotify('success', result.message);
      setTimeout(() => {
        setIsSuccess(false);
        setContent('');
        onClose();
      }, 1600);
    } else {
      setErrorMsg(result.message);
      if (onNotify) onNotify('error', result.message);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#111625] border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[92vh]">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0c101d] shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Radio className="w-4 h-4 text-sky-400" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-wide text-white uppercase font-mono">
              Archival Dispatch
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close dispatch modal"
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800/80 transition-all active:scale-95"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3.5 overflow-y-auto text-xs text-slate-300 flex-1">
          {isSuccess ? (
            <div className="py-8 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center space-y-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-10 h-10 text-emerald-400 animate-bounce" />
              <span className="font-bold text-slate-100 text-sm">Transmission Received</span>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 max-w-[240px]">
                Your submission has logged to the private archival queue for daily ingestion.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 space-y-1">
                <span className="font-bold text-sky-300 block text-xs">
                  Anonymous Feedback & Content
                </span>
                <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                  Propose new vocabulary pools, suggest visual cutouts, or report generator quirks. Zero personal identity or emails are collected.
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Category Dropdown */}
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <label className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wide block">
                  Transmission Category
                </label>
                <select
                  value={category}
                  onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-sky-500"
                >
                  {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
                    <option key={cat} value={cat}>
                      {cat}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              {/* Content Textarea */}
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <label className="text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-slate-300 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Suggestion / Content
                  </label>
                  <span className={`text-[10px] font-mono ${content.length > 450 ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`}>
                    {content.length}/500
                  </span>
                </div>
                <textarea
                  value={content}
                  onChange={(e) => setContent(e.target.value.slice(0, 500))}
                  placeholder="e.g. Word suggestions: 'Recursive', 'Bureaucracy', 'Spork'. Or a pack idea..."
                  rows={4}
                  required
                  className="w-full p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-sky-500 resize-none leading-relaxed placeholder:text-slate-600"
                />
              </div>

              {/* Error Notice */}
              {errorMsg && (
                <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-rose-950/30 border border-rose-900/60 text-rose-300 text-[10.5px] flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{errorMsg}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Device ID / Privacy Indicator */}
              <div className="pt-1 flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500">
                <span>IDENTITY: ANONYMOUS</span>
                <span>INSTALL #{shortId}</span>
              </div>

              {/* Submit Button */}
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={isSubmitting || !content.trim()}
                className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold text-slate-950 bg-sky-400 hover:bg-sky-300 disabled:bg-slate-800 disabled:text-slate-600 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm active:scale-95"
              >
                {isSubmitting ? (
                  <span>Transmitting...</span>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Transmit Dispatch</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </form>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="px-4 py-2 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#0c101d] flex items-center justify-end shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-3.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 transition-all active:scale-95 border border-slate-700"
          >
            Close
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

import React from 'react';

interface InfoModalProps {

export const InfoModal: React.FC<InfoModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams({
