<file path="src/components/SleeveZoomModal.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { X, RotateCw, Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';
import { VinylSleeve } from './VinylSleeve';

interface SleeveZoomModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  album: AlbumEntry | null;
  initialFlipped?: boolean;
}

export const SleeveZoomModal: React.FC<SleeveZoomModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  album,
  initialFlipped = false,
}) => {
  const [isFlipped, setIsFlipped] = useState(initialFlipped);
  const [zoom, setZoom] = useState(1.0);
  const [pan, setPan] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);

  const dragStartRef = useRef({
    x: 0,
    y: 0,
    panX: 0,
    panY: 0,
  });

  // Reset viewport state when modal opens
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isOpen) {
      setIsFlipped(initialFlipped);
      setZoom(1.0);
      setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
      setIsDragging(false);
    }
  }, [isOpen, initialFlipped]);

  // Close on Escape key
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    if (isOpen) window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  // Maximum allowed panning distance computed dynamically from zoom level
  const getMaxPan = (z: number) => {
    if (z <= 1.0) return 0;
    return (340 * z) / 2 + 80;
  };

  const handleZoomChange = (newZoom: number) => {
    const clampedZoom = Math.max(1.0, Math.min(2.5, parseFloat(newZoom.toFixed(2))));
    setZoom(clampedZoom);

    if (clampedZoom <= 1.0) {
      setPan({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    } else {
      const maxP = getMaxPan(clampedZoom);
      setPan((prev) => ({
        x: Math.max(-maxP, Math.min(maxP, prev.x)),
        y: Math.max(-maxP, Math.min(maxP, prev.y)),
      }));
    }
  };

  // --- MOUSE DRAG PANNING ---
  const handleMouseDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (zoom <= 1.0) return;
    setIsDragging(true);
    dragStartRef.current = {
      x: e.clientX,
      y: e.clientY,
      panX: pan.x,
      panY: pan.y,
    };
  };

  const handleMouseMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!isDragging) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const dx = e.clientX - dragStartRef.current.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - dragStartRef.current.y;

    const maxPan = getMaxPan(zoom);
    setPan({
      x: Math.max(-maxPan, Math.min(maxPan, dragStartRef.current.panX + dx)),
      y: Math.max(-maxPan, Math.min(maxPan, dragStartRef.current.panY + dy)),
    });
  };

  const handleMouseUp = () => setIsDragging(false);

  // --- TOUCH PANNING (Mobile Simulation) ---
  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (zoom <= 1.0 || e.touches.length !== 1) return;
    const touch = e.touches[0];
    setIsDragging(true);
    dragStartRef.current = {
      x: touch.clientX,
      y: touch.clientY,
      panX: pan.x,
      panY: pan.y,
    };
  };

  const handleTouchMove = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (!isDragging || e.touches.length !== 1) return;
    const touch = e.touches[0];
    const dx = touch.clientX - dragStartRef.current.x;
    const dy = touch.clientY - dragStartRef.current.y;

    const maxPan = getMaxPan(zoom);
    setPan({
      x: Math.max(-maxPan, Math.min(maxPan, dragStartRef.current.panX + dx)),
      y: Math.max(-maxPan, Math.min(maxPan, dragStartRef.current.panY + dy)),
    });
  };

  // --- MOUSE WHEEL PANNING ---
  const handleWheel = (e: React.WheelEvent) => {
    if (zoom <= 1.0) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const maxPan = getMaxPan(zoom);
    setPan((prev) => ({
      x: Math.max(-maxPan, Math.min(maxPan, prev.x - e.deltaX)),
      y: Math.max(-maxPan, Math.min(maxPan, prev.y - e.deltaY)),
    }));
  };

  if (!isOpen || !album) return null;

  const isZoomed = zoom > 1.0;
  const zoomPercent = Math.round(zoom * 100);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/95 backdrop-blur-md flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-3 select-none">
      {/* Top Header Controls Bar */}
      <div className="w-full max-w-xl flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 py-1.5 z-20 shrink-0 border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          {/* Flip Toggle Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsFlipped(!isFlipped)}
            className="h-8 px-2.5 rounded-md bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-700 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm active:scale-95 transition-all"
          >
            <RotateCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
            <span>{isFlipped ? 'Front View' : 'Back Notes'}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Quick Preset Buttons */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-900/80 p-0.5 rounded-md border border-slate-700/80 text-[10px] font-mono">
            {[100, 150, 200, 250].map((pct) => (
              <button
                key={pct}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleZoomChange(pct / 100)}
                className={`px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded transition-all ${
                  zoomPercent === pct
                    ? 'bg-orange-500 text-slate-950 font-bold'
                    : 'text-stone-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800'
                }`}
              >
                {pct}%
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Stepper with Numeric Input */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700/80 rounded-md h-8 px-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleZoomChange(zoom - 0.1)}
              disabled={zoom <= 1.0}
              className="w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 active:scale-90"
              aria-label="Zoom Out"
            >
              <Minus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center px-1">
              <input
                type="number"
                min="100"
                max="250"
                step="5"
                value={zoomPercent}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const val = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);
                  if (!isNaN(val)) handleZoomChange(val / 100);
                }}
                className="w-12 bg-transparent text-center font-mono text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 focus:outline-none"
              />
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-stone-400 select-none">%</span>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleZoomChange(zoom + 0.1)}
              disabled={zoom >= 2.5}
              className="w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center text-stone-400 hover:text-white disabled:opacity-30 active:scale-90"
              aria-label="Zoom In"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Close Modal Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close Zoom View"
            className="h-8 w-8 rounded-md bg-slate-800/90 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-white border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-center transition-all active:scale-95 shrink-0"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Pan / Canvas Stage */}
      <div
        onMouseDown={handleMouseDown}
        onMouseMove={handleMouseMove}
        onMouseUp={handleMouseUp}
        onMouseLeave={handleMouseUp}
        onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
        onTouchMove={handleTouchMove}
        onTouchEnd={handleMouseUp}
        onWheel={handleWheel}
        className={`flex-1 w-full overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center relative ${
          isZoomed
            ? isDragging
              ? 'cursor-grabbing'
              : 'cursor-grab'
            : 'cursor-default'
        }`}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            transform: `translate3d(${pan.x}px, ${pan.y}px, 0px) scale(${zoom})`,
            transformOrigin: 'center center',
            transition: isDragging ? 'none' : 'transform 0.12s ease-out',
          }}
          className="w-[340px] h-[340px] shrink-0 pointer-events-auto select-none"
        >
          {/* Passing a no-op to onToggleFlip prevents sleeve clicks from triggering flips while panning */}
          <VinylSleeve
            album={album}
            isFlipped={isFlipped}
            isLoading={false}
            onToggleFlip={() => {}}
            hideZoomButton
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Bottom Status / Navigation Hint */}
      <p className="text-[10px] text-stone-400 font-mono py-1 pointer-events-none text-center shrink-0">
        {isZoomed
          ? 'Drag to pan • Use Front View / Back Notes button above to flip'
          : 'Tap preset (150%, 200%) or +/- to inspect artwork and liner notes'}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/SubHeaderNav.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Disc3 } from 'lucide-react';
import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';

interface SubHeaderNavProps {
  entries: AlbumEntry[];
  currentIndex: number;
  onPrev: () => void;
  onNext: () => void;
}

export const SubHeaderNav: React.FC<SubHeaderNavProps> = ({
  entries,
  currentIndex,
  onPrev,
  onNext,
}) => {
  const current = entries[currentIndex];

  const formatDate = (timestamp?: number) => {
    if (!timestamp) return "Today's Selection";
    const d = new Date(timestamp);
    return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, {
      month: 'short',
      day: 'numeric',
      year: 'numeric',
    });
  };

  return (
    <nav className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-1 bg-slate-900/60 border-b border-slate-800/60 select-none">
      <button
        onClick={onPrev}
        disabled={entries.length <= 1 || currentIndex <= 0}
        aria-label="Previous record"
        className="p-1 rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-orange-400 disabled:opacity-25 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-all active:scale-90"
        title="Previous Album"
      >
        <ChevronLeft className="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>

      {/* Dynamic Carousel representation without static counters like 'X of 28' */}
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center justify-center min-w-0 px-2">
        {current ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-300 font-medium truncate max-w-full">
            <Disc3 className="w-3 h-3 text-orange-400 shrink-0" />
            <span className="truncate text-amber-200/90 font-mono tracking-tight text-[11px]">
              {formatDate(current.timestamp)} • {current.year} Pressing
            </span>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-mono tracking-tight">
            Ready for your daily drop
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Dynamic position dots */}
        {entries.length > 1 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 max-w-[120px] overflow-hidden justify-center">
            {entries.slice(Math.max(0, currentIndex - 3), currentIndex + 4).map((_, idx) => {
              const actualIdx = Math.max(0, currentIndex - 3) + idx;
              const isActive = actualIdx === currentIndex;
              return (
                <div
                  key={actualIdx}
                  className={`h-1 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ${
                    isActive ? 'w-3.5 bg-orange-400' : 'w-1 bg-slate-600/70'
                  }`}
                />
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <button
        onClick={onNext}
        disabled={entries.length <= 1 || currentIndex >= entries.length - 1}
        aria-label="Next record"
        className="p-1 rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-orange-400 disabled:opacity-25 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-all active:scale-90"
        title="Next Album"
      >
        <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4" />
      </button>
    </nav>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/TechnicianModal.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  X, 
  Wrench, 
  Download, 
  Trash2, 
  Clock, 
  Radio, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  AlertCircle,
  Smartphone,
  Layers,
  RotateCcw
} from 'lucide-react';
import { performFactoryReset } from '../services/storageService';

interface TechnicianModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  isStandalone: boolean;
  onTriggerInstall: () => void;
  onResetCooldown: () => void;
  onNotify: (type: 'success' | 'info' | 'error', text: string) => void;
}

export const TechnicianModal: React.FC<TechnicianModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  isStandalone,
  onTriggerInstall,
  onResetCooldown,
  onNotify,
}) => {
  const [swCount, setSwCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [cacheKeys, setCacheKeys] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [hasPrompt, setHasPrompt] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [confirmWipe, setConfirmWipe] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) {
      setConfirmWipe(false);
      return;
    }

    setHasPrompt(Boolean((window as any).deferredPrompt));

    if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
      navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistrations().then((regs) => {
        setSwCount(regs.length);
      });
    }

    if ('caches' in window) {
      caches.keys().then((keys) => {
        setCacheKeys(keys);
      });
    }
  }, [isOpen]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] flex items-center justify-center p-3 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#161310] border-2 border-amber-600/70 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-amber-900/40 bg-[#0e0c0a] shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Wrench className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-mono font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-200">
                Technician Service Deck
              </h3>
              <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-amber-500/70 block -mt-0.5">
                PWA Lifecycle &amp; QA Diagnostics
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition-all active:scale-95"
            aria-label="Close technician panel"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Diagnostics & Operations Body */}
        <div className="p-3.5 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto text-xs font-mono text-slate-300 flex-1">
          {/* Live Device / PWA Telemetry */}
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-black/60 border border-slate-800 space-y-1.5">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-400 block uppercase">
              System Telemetry:
            </span>
            <div className="space-y-1 text-[10px]">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-400">Display Mode:</span>
                <span className={isStandalone ? 'text-emerald-400 font-bold' : 'text-amber-400 font-bold'}>
                  {isStandalone ? 'STANDALONE (INSTALLED)' : 'BROWSER (TAB)'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-400">Install Prompt:</span>
                <span className={hasPrompt ? 'text-emerald-400 font-bold' : 'text-rose-400 font-bold'}>
                  {hasPrompt ? 'READY (UNCACHED)' : 'SUPPRESSED / UNAVAILABLE'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-400">Service Workers:</span>
                <span className="text-slate-200">{swCount} active</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="text-slate-400">CacheStorage Buckets:</span>
                <span className="text-slate-200">{cacheKeys.length} keys</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* QA Actions */}
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider block">
              Test Operations:
            </span>

            {/* Trigger Install Prompt */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                if ((window as any).deferredPrompt) {
                  onTriggerInstall();
                } else {
                  onNotify('info', 'Chrome is suppressing automated prompt. Test menu install via three dots (⋮).');
                }
              }}
              className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-lg bg-emerald-950/60 hover:bg-emerald-900/60 border border-emerald-600/60 text-emerald-300 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between transition-all active:scale-98"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Fire Install Prompt</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[9px] opacity-75">{hasPrompt ? 'ARMED' : 'SUPPRESSED'}</span>
            </button>

            {/* Reset 24-Hour Cooldown */}
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onResetCooldown();
                onNotify('success', 'Daily Drop cooldown reset to 0:00.');
              }}
              className="w-full py-2 px-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-bold text-[11px] flex items-center justify-between transition-all active:scale-98"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400" />
                <span>Zero Daily Drop Cooldown</span>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[9px] text-amber-400">INSTANT MINT</span>
            </button>

            {/* Nuclear Wipe */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-950/30 border border-rose-900/50 space-y-1.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-rose-400 font-bold text-[10px] uppercase">
                <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
                <span>Nuclear Purge &amp; Reset</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[9.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                Deletes all IndexedDB, CacheStorage, localStorage, unregisters workers, and force-reloads.
              </p>
              {!confirmWipe ? (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setConfirmWipe(true)}
                  className="w-full py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-900/40 hover:bg-rose-900/60 border border-rose-700/60 text-rose-200 font-bold text-[10px] uppercase transition-all active:scale-95"
                >
                  Execute Nuclear Purge
                </button>
              ) : (
                <div className="flex gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setConfirmWipe(false)}
                    className="flex-1 py-1 rounded bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 text-[10px]"
                  >
                    Cancel
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => performFactoryReset()}
                    className="flex-1 py-1 rounded bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold text-[10px]"
                  >
                    Confirm Wipe
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="px-4 py-2 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#0e0c0a] flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
          <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-500">DEV TOOL v1.0</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono font-semibold bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 transition-all active:scale-95 border border-slate-700"
          >
            Exit Deck
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/Toast.tsx">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { ToastMessage } from '../types';
import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, Info, X, Copy, Check } from 'lucide-react';

interface ToastProps {
  toasts: ToastMessage[];
  onDismiss: (id: string) => void;
}

export const Toast: React.FC<ToastProps> = ({ toasts, onDismiss }) => {
  const [copiedId, setCopiedId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  const handleCopy = (id: string, text: string) => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    setCopiedId(id);
    setTimeout(() => {
      setCopiedId(null);
    }, 2000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed top-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-full max-w-sm px-3 z-50 pointer-events-none space-y-2">
      {toasts.map((toast) => {
        const isError = toast.type === 'error';
        const isSuccess = toast.type === 'success';

        return (
          <div
            key={toast.id}
            role="alert"
            className={`pointer-events-auto w-full p-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border backdrop-blur-md flex items-start gap-2.5 transition-all duration-200 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 ${
              isError
                ? 'bg-rose-950/90 border-rose-500/60 text-rose-100 shadow-rose-950/50'
                : isSuccess
                ? 'bg-emerald-950/90 border-emerald-500/60 text-emerald-100 shadow-emerald-950/50'
                : 'bg-slate-900/90 border-slate-700/80 text-slate-100 shadow-black/60'
            }`}
          >
            {/* Icon */}
            <div className="shrink-0 mt-0.5">
              {isError && <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400" />}
              {isSuccess && <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400" />}
              {!isError && !isSuccess && <Info className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />}
            </div>

            {/* Content text */}
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              {isError && (
                <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-rose-300 uppercase tracking-wider mb-0.5">
                  Inspection Error
                </div>
              )}
              <p className="text-xs break-words font-mono leading-relaxed select-text">
                {toast.text}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Actions: Copy for error, Dismiss for all */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 ml-1">
              {isError && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleCopy(toast.id, toast.text)}
                  className="p-1 rounded-md bg-rose-900/60 hover:bg-rose-800/80 text-rose-300 hover:text-white transition-all active:scale-90"
                  title="Copy error string"
                  aria-label="Copy error text"
                >
                  {copiedId === toast.id ? (
                    <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
                  ) : (
                    <Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  )}
                </button>
              )}

              <button
                onClick={() => onDismiss(toast.id)}
                className="p-1 rounded-md text-slate-400 hover:text-white transition-all active:scale-90"
                aria-label="Dismiss notification"
              >
                <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

/**

 * Every single entry has an entirely unique combination of [Backdrop, Subject, Modifier, Palette]
 * to ensure zero visual duplication across the starter crate.

import {
  AlbumEntry,
  CanvasRecipe,
  HypeSticker,

  TitleShadowStyle,
  TitleComposition,
} from '../types';
import { gagCanvasEngine } from '../services/gagCanvasEngine';

export interface StarterCrateDefinition {

  albumTitle: string;

}

/**
 * Initializes and returns the full starter crate with rendered canvas covers
 */
export function getStarterCrateAlbums(): AlbumEntry[] {
  return STARTER_CRATE_DEFINITIONS.map((item, idx) => {
    // Render the procedural gag cover
    const coverImageUrl = gagCanvasEngine.renderCover(
      item.bandName,
      item.albumTitle,
      item.recipe
    );

    return {
      id: item.id,
      bandName: item.bandName,
      albumTitle: item.albumTitle,
      year: item.year,
      catalogNumber: item.catalogNumber,
      artDirectorPrompt: `Visual gag cover in Volume One aesthetic: ${item.bandName} - ${item.albumTitle}`,
      tracks: item.tracks,
      fauxReviews: item.fauxReviews,
      bandBio: item.bandBio,
      coverImageUrl,
      timestamp: Date.now() - (STARTER_CRATE_DEFINITIONS.length - idx) * 86400000,
      isFavorite: false,
      skewDeg: item.skewDeg,
      stickers: item.stickers,
      recipe: item.recipe,
      audioPreset: item.audioPreset,
      fontFamily: item.fontFamily,
      textColor: item.textColor,
      shadowStyle: item.shadowStyle,
      titleLayout: item.titleLayout,
    };
  });
}

<file path="src/services/feedbackService.ts">
const FORM_ENDPOINT =
  'https://docs.google.com/forms/d/e/1FAIpQLSexZMDE-rpOhyHfHyr4WjqYcd8Lju6yDMeeFwo6snzrVzZuNg/formResponse';

const ENTRY_CATEGORY = 'entry.1684451577';
const ENTRY_CONTENT = 'entry.530337516';
const ENTRY_VERSION = 'entry.1547053940';
const ENTRY_DEVICE_ID = 'entry.2002752014';

const COOLDOWN_MS = 3 * 60 * 1000; // 3-minute cooldown between dispatches

/**
 * Retrieves or initializes an anonymous, non-identifying installation UUID.
 */
export function getAnonymousDeviceId(): string {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return 'unknown-device';
  let id = localStorage.getItem('ydrbn_device_uuid');
  if (!id) {
    id = typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && crypto.randomUUID
      ? crypto.randomUUID()
      : 'dev-' + Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 10);
    localStorage.setItem('ydrbn_device_uuid', id);
  }
  return id;
}

export interface DispatchResult {
  success: boolean;
  message: string;
}

/**
 * Sends feedback silently in the background using no-cors fetch.
 */
export async function transmitDispatch(
  category: string,
  content: string,
  appVersion: string = 'v1.0.0'
): Promise<DispatchResult> {
  if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && !navigator.onLine) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Network offline. Please connect to transmit your dispatch.',
    };
  }

  const trimmed = content.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Dispatch content cannot be blank.' };
  }

  if (trimmed.length > 500) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: 'Dispatch exceeds maximum limit of 500 characters.',
    };
  }

  // Throttling check
  const lastSent = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('ydrbn_last_dispatch') || '0', 10);
  const now = Date.now();
  if (now - lastSent < COOLDOWN_MS) {
    const remainingSec = Math.ceil((COOLDOWN_MS - (now - lastSent)) / 1000);
    return {
      success: false,
      message: `Transmission throttle active. Please wait ${remainingSec}s.`,
    };
  }

  const deviceId = getAnonymousDeviceId();

  const formData = new URLSearchParams();
  formData.append(ENTRY_CATEGORY, category);
  formData.append(ENTRY_CONTENT, trimmed);
  formData.append(ENTRY_VERSION, appVersion);
  formData.append(ENTRY_DEVICE_ID, deviceId);

  try {
    await fetch(FORM_ENDPOINT, {
      method: 'POST',
      mode: 'no-cors',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
      },
      body: formData.toString(),
    });

    localStorage.setItem('ydrbn_last_dispatch', now.toString());
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Dispatch transmitted successfully to the archive.',
    };
  } catch (error: any) {
    return {
      success: false,
      message: `Transmission failed: ${error?.message || 'Check network'}`,
    };
  }
}
</file>

  { date: '8/13', city: 'Castle Rock, ME' },
  { date: '8/18', city: 'Derry, ME' },
  { date: '8/19', city: 'Derry, ME' },
  { date: '8/20', city: 'Hogsmeade, UK' },
  { date: '8/25', city: 'Hogsmeade, UK' },
  { date: '8/26', city: 'Bedrock, IE' },
  { date: '8/27', city: 'Bedrock, IE' },
];

export interface TourRenderOptions {
  stops?: TourStop[];
  accentColor?: string;
  showDividingLine?: boolean;
}

export function renderTourBackGraphic(options: TourRenderOptions = {}): string {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = 3600;
  canvas.height = 4800;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  if (!ctx) return '';

  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);

  const ink = options.accentColor || '#8b1410';
  const stops = options.stops && options.stops.length > 0 ? options.stops : CANONICAL_TOUR_ROSTER;
  const half = Math.ceil(stops.length / 2);
  const leftCol = stops.slice(0, half);
  const rightCol = stops.slice(half);

  // 1. Center Vertical Rule (Center is X = 1800)
  if (options.showDividingLine !== false) {
    ctx.strokeStyle = ink;
    ctx.lineWidth = 14;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(1800, 360);
    ctx.lineTo(1800, 4440);
    ctx.stroke();
  }

  // 2. Typography Geometry
  const startY = 490;
  const rowCount = Math.max(leftCol.length, rightCol.length);
  const availableHeight = 3900;
  const lineHeight = availableHeight / rowCount;

  ctx.fillStyle = ink;
  ctx.font = 'bold italic 120px "Playfair Display", Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  // Left Column (Max width stops before 1800)
  leftCol.forEach((item, i) => {
    const y = startY + i * lineHeight;
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillText(item.date, 150, y);
    ctx.fillText(item.city, 550, y, 1150); // Hard boundary at 1700
  });

  // Right Column (Max width stops before 3600)
  rightCol.forEach((item, i) => {
    const y = startY + i * lineHeight;
    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
    ctx.fillText(item.date, 1950, y);
    ctx.fillText(item.city, 2350, y, 1150); // Hard boundary at 3500
  });

  // 3. Tiny Manufacturer Mark at the Bottom
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.font = 'bold 60px "Space Mono", monospace';
  ctx.fillText('Y.D.R.B.N.', 1800, 4650);

  return canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
}

def write_json(path, data):
    with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

print("🚀 Engaging Warp Drive... Building App Store Folders!")

# Ensure base directory exists
os.makedirs(BASE_DIR, exist_ok=True)

# Write Master Index
write_json(os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "index.json"), MASTER_INDEX)

# Build Word Packs
for folder, data in WORD_PACKS.items():
    pack_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, folder)
    os.makedirs(pack_dir, exist_ok=True)
    write_json(os.path.join(pack_dir, "manifest.json"), data["manifest"])
    write_json(os.path.join(pack_dir, "words.json"), data["words"])
    print(f"✅ Created Word Pack: {folder}")

# Build Image Packs
for folder, data in IMAGE_PACKS.items():
    pack_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, folder)
    stamps_dir = os.path.join(pack_dir, "stamps")
    os.makedirs(stamps_dir, exist_ok=True)
    write_json(os.path.join(pack_dir, "manifest.json"), data["manifest"])
    print(f"✅ Created Image Pack: {folder} (Drop images into {stamps_dir})")

print("🎉 Great Scott! The Holodeck is fully loaded.")

<file path="src/components/ActionButtonsRow.tsx">
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { RotateCw, Music2, Square } from 'lucide-react';
import { audioSynthesizer } from '../services/audioSynthesizer';

interface ActionButtonsRowProps {
  isFlipped: boolean;
  onToggleFlip: () => void;
  isPlayingAudio: boolean;
  onPlayAudio: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export const ActionButtonsRow: React.FC<ActionButtonsRowProps> = ({
  isFlipped,
  onToggleFlip,
  isPlayingAudio,
  onPlayAudio,
  disabled = false,
}) => {
  const [levels, setLevels] = useState<{ left: number; right: number }>({ left: 0, right: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlayingAudio) {
      setLevels({ left: 0, right: 0 });
      return;
    }

    let rafId: number;
    const updateMeter = () => {
      setLevels(audioSynthesizer.getAudioLevels());
      rafId = requestAnimationFrame(updateMeter);
    };
    rafId = requestAnimationFrame(updateMeter);

    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
  }, [isPlayingAudio]);

  const renderLedChannel = (label: string, level: number) => {
    const activeCount = Math.round(level * 10);
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
        <span className="text-[8px] font-mono font-bold text-amber-500/80 w-2">{label}</span>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-[2px]">
          {Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => {
            const isLit = i < activeCount;
            const colorClass =
              i >= 8
                ? isLit
                  ? 'bg-rose-500 shadow-[0_0_5px_#f43f5e]'
                  : 'bg-rose-950/40'
                : i >= 6
                ? isLit
                  ? 'bg-amber-400 shadow-[0_0_5px_#f59e0b]'
                  : 'bg-amber-950/40'
                : isLit
                ? 'bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_5px_#10b981]'
                : 'bg-emerald-950/30';
            return <span key={i} className={`w-1.5 h-2 rounded-[1px] transition-colors duration-75 ${colorClass}`} />;
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full px-3 py-1 space-y-1.5 z-20">
      <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        {/* Button 1: [↺ Back Sleeve & Tracks / Front Cover] */}
        <button
          onClick={onToggleFlip}
          disabled={disabled}
          className={`btn-chassis flex-1 h-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-md text-[11px] tracking-wider border cursor-pointer ${
            isFlipped
              ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/50'
              : 'bg-slate-900 text-slate-200 border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-800 hover:text-white'
          }`}
        >
          <RotateCw
            className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 transition-transform duration-300 shrink-0 ${
              isFlipped ? 'rotate-180 text-amber-400' : 'text-amber-500/80'
            }`}
          />
          <span className="truncate">{isFlipped ? 'Front Cover' : 'Flip Sleeve'}</span>
        </button>

        {/* Button 2: [♫ Create Audio Clip From Album] */}
        <button
          onClick={onPlayAudio}
          disabled={disabled}
          className={`btn-chassis flex-1 h-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-md text-[11px] tracking-wider border cursor-pointer ${
            isPlayingAudio
              ? 'bg-orange-500 text-slate-950 font-black border-orange-300 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(249,115,22,0.4)]'
              : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-600 to-orange-700 text-slate-950 font-black border-amber-400/50 hover:from-amber-500 hover:to-orange-600'
          }`}
        >
          {isPlayingAudio ? (
            <>
              <Square className="w-3 h-3 fill-current animate-pulse shrink-0" />
              <span className="truncate">Stop Turntable</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Music2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-950 shrink-0" />
              <span className="truncate">Spin Audio Clip</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Live Recessed Stereo VU Peak Meter Bar (Reveals smoothly when audio plays) */}
      {isPlayingAudio && (
        <div className="well-inset w-full px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md flex items-center justify-between animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-red-500 animate-ping" />
            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono uppercase tracking-widest text-amber-300/90 font-bold">
              33⅓ RPM // OUTPUT LEVEL
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {renderLedChannel('L', levels.left)}
            {renderLedChannel('R', levels.right)}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/ContentPackSection.tsx">
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { 
  Package, 
  Trash2, 
  CheckCircle2, 
  RefreshCw, 
  Power, 
  BookOpen, 
  Image as ImageIcon, 
  Shapes,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  Hammer,
  Edit3,
  Download,
  Plus
} from 'lucide-react';
import { 
  getAvailablePacks, 
  checkPackStatus, 
  installCurrentPack, 
  uninstallCurrentPack,
  getCustomPacks,
  saveCustomPack,
  deleteCustomPack,
  exportCustomPackAsJson
} from '../services/contentPackService';
import { StoredPack } from '../services/packStorage';
import { getPreferences, togglePackStatus } from '../services/preferencesService';
import { hydrateContentPools } from '../services/proceduralGenerator';
import { CrateForgeModal, CustomPackBundle } from './CrateForgeModal';

export const ContentPackSection: React.FC = () => {
  const [packs, setPacks] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [customPacks, setCustomPacks] = useState<StoredPack[]>([]);
  const [statuses, setStatuses] = useState<Record<string, any>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [installingSection, setInstallingSection] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState(getPreferences());

  // Crate Forge Modal state
  const [isForgeOpen, setIsForgeOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingPackBundle, setEditingPackBundle] = useState<CustomPackBundle | null>(null);

  const [expandedSections, setExpandedSections] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({
    custom: true,
    words: false,
    subjects: false,
    backgrounds: false,
  });

  const toggleSection = (sectionKey: string) => {
    setExpandedSections((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [sectionKey]: !prev[sectionKey],
    }));
  };

  const refreshStatus = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [available, localCustom] = await Promise.all([
        getAvailablePacks().catch(() => []),
        getCustomPacks(),
      ]);

      setPacks(available);
      setCustomPacks(localCustom);

      const statusMap: Record<string, any> = {};
      for (const p of available) {
        statusMap[p.id] = await checkPackStatus(p.id, p.manifestUrl);
      }
      setStatuses(statusMap);
      setPrefs(getPreferences());
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    refreshStatus();
  }, []);

  const handleTogglePack = async (packId: string, currentlyEnabled: boolean) => {
    togglePackStatus(packId, !currentlyEnabled);
    setPrefs(getPreferences());
    await hydrateContentPools();
  };

  const handleOpenForgeNew = () => {
    setEditingPackBundle(null);
    setIsForgeOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenForgeEdit = (pack: StoredPack) => {
    if (pack.customBundle) {
      setEditingPackBundle(pack.customBundle);
    } else {
      // Reconstruct bundle if needed
      setEditingPackBundle({
        manifest: {
          id: pack.id,
          name: pack.manifest.name,
          description: pack.manifest.description || '',
          version: pack.installedVersion || '1.0.0',
          author: pack.manifest.author || 'Archivist',
          isCustom: true,
          createdAt: pack.installedAt,
        },
        words: {
          nouns: pack.words?.nouns || [],
          adjectives: pack.words?.adjectives || [],
          ensembles: pack.words?.ensembles || [],
        },
        stamps: [],
      });
    }
    setIsForgeOpen(true);
  };

  const handleDeleteCustomPack = async (packId: string) => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await deleteCustomPack(packId);
      await refreshStatus();
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveCustomBundle = async (bundle: CustomPackBundle) => {
    await saveCustomPack(bundle);
    await refreshStatus();
  };

  const handleToggleInstallAll = async (sectionKey: string, sectionPacks: any[], allInstalled: boolean) => {
    if (loading || installingSection) return;
    setInstallingSection(sectionKey);
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      if (allInstalled) {
        for (const p of sectionPacks) {
          if (statuses[p.id]?.isInstalled) {
            await uninstallCurrentPack(p.id);
          }
        }
      } else {
        for (const p of sectionPacks) {
          if (!statuses[p.id]?.isInstalled || statuses[p.id]?.hasUpdate) {
            await installCurrentPack(p.manifestUrl);
          }
        }
      }
      await refreshStatus();
      await hydrateContentPools();
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
      setInstallingSection(null);
    }
  };

  const wordPacks = packs.filter((p) => p.id.includes('word') || p.words);
  const subjectPacks = packs.filter((p) => p.id.includes('subject') || p.subjects);
  const bgPacks = packs.filter(
    (p) => !wordPacks.some((wp) => wp.id === p.id) && !subjectPacks.some((sp) => sp.id === p.id)
  );

  const renderPackCard = (pack: any) => {
    const status = statuses[pack.id];
    const isInstalled = status?.isInstalled;
    const isEnabled = !prefs.disabledPacks.includes(pack.id);

    const descMatch = (pack.description || '').match(/^(\d+)(.*)/);
    const formattedDescription = descMatch ? (
      <>
        <span className="font-bold text-slate-100">{descMatch[1]}</span>
        {descMatch[2]}
      </>
    ) : (
      pack.description
    );

    return (
      <div 
        key={pack.id} 
        className={`p-3 rounded-xl border overflow-hidden transition-all ${
          isInstalled && isEnabled 
            ? 'bg-slate-900/90 border-slate-700/80 shadow-sm' 
            : 'bg-slate-950/60 border-slate-800/70'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-2 text-xs">
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-snug flex-1">
            {formattedDescription}
          </p>
          <div className="shrink-0 text-right pt-0.5">
            {isInstalled ? (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-400 font-mono text-[10px]">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" /> v{status.installedVersion}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-slate-500 italic text-[11px]">Not installed</span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 w-full">
          {!isInstalled && (
            <button
              onClick={async () => {
                setLoading(true);
                await installCurrentPack(pack.manifestUrl);
                await refreshStatus();
              }}
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-2 px-3 bg-[#d97706] hover:bg-[#b45309] text-slate-950 font-bold rounded-lg transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm text-center shadow-md active:scale-[0.99] leading-tight truncate"
            >
              {pack.name}
            </button>
          )}

          {isInstalled && status?.hasUpdate && (
            <button
              onClick={async () => {
                setLoading(true);
                await installCurrentPack(pack.manifestUrl);
                await refreshStatus();
              }}
              disabled={loading}
              className="w-full py-2 px-3 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-bold rounded-lg transition-all text-xs sm:text-sm text-center shadow-md active:scale-[0.99] leading-tight truncate"
            >
              Update: {pack.name}
            </button>
          )}

          {isInstalled && !status?.hasUpdate && (
            <>
              <button
                onClick={() => handleTogglePack(pack.id, isEnabled)}
                className={`flex-1 min-w-0 py-2 px-2.5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm transition-all leading-tight ${
                  isEnabled
                    ? 'bg-emerald-950/70 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-600/60 hover:bg-emerald-900/70 shadow-sm'
                    : 'bg-slate-800/80 text-slate-400 border border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-700/80'
                }`}
                title={isEnabled ? `Click to disable ${pack.name}` : `Click to enable ${pack.name}`}
              >
                <Power className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 ${isEnabled ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`} />
                <span className="truncate">{pack.name}</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={async () => {
                  setLoading(true);
                  await uninstallCurrentPack(pack.id);
                  await refreshStatus();
                }}
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center bg-rose-950/30 text-rose-400 border border-rose-900/40 rounded-lg hover:bg-rose-900/50 transition-colors shrink-0"
                title={`Uninstall ${pack.name} from storage`}
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              </button>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  const renderCustomPackCard = (pack: StoredPack) => {
    const isEnabled = !prefs.disabledPacks.includes(pack.id);
    const nounCount = pack.words?.nouns?.length || 0;
    const adjCount = pack.words?.adjectives?.length || 0;
    const stampCount = pack.manifest?.subjects?.length || 0;

    return (
      <div
        key={pack.id}
        className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border overflow-hidden transition-all ${
          isEnabled
            ? 'bg-slate-900/90 border-amber-500/40 shadow-sm'
            : 'bg-slate-950/60 border-slate-800/70'
        }`}
      >
        {/* Row 1: Name on left, inline count badges + version on right */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-2 text-xs">
          <span className="font-bold text-amber-300 text-xs truncate flex-1">
            {pack.manifest.name}
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0 text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400">
            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800 text-slate-300">
              {nounCount}N · {adjCount}A{stampCount > 0 ? ` · ${stampCount}C` : ''}
            </span>
            <span className="text-amber-400/90">v{pack.installedVersion}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Row 2: Compact Action Controls */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 w-full">
          <button
            onClick={() => handleTogglePack(pack.id, isEnabled)}
            className={`flex-1 min-w-0 py-1 px-2.5 h-7 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold text-[11px] transition-all leading-tight ${
              isEnabled
                ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/40 hover:bg-amber-500/30'
                : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-500 border border-slate-700'
            }`}
          >
            <Power className={`w-3 h-3 shrink-0 ${isEnabled ? 'text-amber-400' : 'text-slate-500'}`} />
            <span>{isEnabled ? 'MOUNTED' : 'OFFLINE'}</span>
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => handleOpenForgeEdit(pack)}
            className="h-7 w-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 border border-slate-700 transition-colors shrink-0"
            title="Edit crate in the Forge"
            aria-label="Edit pack"
          >
            <Edit3 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (pack.customBundle) {
                exportCustomPackAsJson(pack.customBundle);
              }
            }}
            className="h-7 w-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-800/80 hover:bg-slate-700 text-sky-400 border border-slate-700 transition-colors shrink-0"
            title="Download crate bundle JSON"
            aria-label="Export JSON"
          >
            <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => handleDeleteCustomPack(pack.id)}
            className="h-7 w-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-rose-950/30 hover:bg-rose-900/50 text-rose-400 border border-rose-900/40 transition-colors shrink-0"
            title="Delete custom crate"
            aria-label="Delete pack"
          >
            <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  };

  const renderSection = (
    key: string,
    title: string,
    sectionPacks: any[],
    Icon: any,
    accentColor: string,
    textColor: string
  ) => {
    if (sectionPacks.length === 0) return null;

    const installedCount = sectionPacks.filter((p) => statuses[p.id]?.isInstalled).length;
    const allInstalled = sectionPacks.length > 0 && installedCount === sectionPacks.length;
    const someInstalled = installedCount > 0;
    const isExpanded = expandedSections[key];
    const isThisSectionProcessing = installingSection === key;

    return (
      <div className="border border-slate-800/90 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-950/40 transition-colors">
        <div 
          onClick={() => toggleSection(key)}
          className="flex items-center justify-between p-2.5 bg-slate-900/80 hover:bg-slate-800/60 cursor-pointer select-none transition-colors"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0 pr-2">
            {isExpanded ? (
              <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-300 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 shrink-0" />
            )}
            <Icon className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${accentColor} shrink-0`} />
            <span className={`text-[11px] font-bold ${textColor} tracking-wider uppercase truncate`}>
              {title}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500 shrink-0">
              ({installedCount}/{sectionPacks.length})
            </span>
          </div>

          <div onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()} className="shrink-0">
            {isThisSectionProcessing ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/40 px-2 py-1 rounded select-none">
                <RefreshCw className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400 animate-spin" />
                <span className="font-mono text-[9px] text-amber-300 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">
                  {allInstalled ? 'Removing...' : 'Installing...'}
                </span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <label 
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] px-2 py-1 rounded border transition-colors select-none ${
                  loading 
                    ? 'opacity-40 pointer-events-none bg-slate-900 border-slate-800 text-slate-600' 
                    : 'text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 cursor-pointer bg-slate-800/70 hover:bg-slate-800 border-slate-700/60'
                }`}
                title={allInstalled ? `Uninstall all ${title}` : `Install all ${title}`}
              >
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={allInstalled}
                  ref={(el) => {
                    if (el) el.indeterminate = someInstalled && !allInstalled;
                  }}
                  onChange={() => handleToggleInstallAll(key, sectionPacks, allInstalled)}
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded accent-amber-500 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                />
                <span className="font-mono text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
                  {allInstalled ? 'All In' : someInstalled ? 'Install Rest' : 'Install All'}
                </span>
              </label>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {isExpanded && (
          <div className="p-2 space-y-2 border-t border-slate-800/60 bg-black/20 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
            {sectionPacks.map(renderPackCard)}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {/* Top Action Card: Launch The Crate Forge */}
      <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-950/40 via-slate-900 to-slate-900 border border-amber-500/40 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-md">
        <div className="space-y-0.5 min-w-0 pr-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Hammer className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
            <span className="font-bold text-slate-100 text-xs">The Crate Forge</span>
          </div>
          <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
            Author custom word lists and transparent cutout stamps directly on your device.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleOpenForgeNew}
          className="py-2 px-3 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-300 text-slate-950 flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all shadow active:scale-95 shrink-0"
        >
          <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>New Crate</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Main Manifest Container */}
      <div className="bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3.5 text-slate-200">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-2.5 border-b border-slate-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Package className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400" />
            <span className="font-semibold text-xs sm:text-sm tracking-wide">Installed Content Manifest</span>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={refreshStatus} 
            disabled={loading} 
            aria-label="Refresh content list"
            className="p-1 hover:bg-slate-800 rounded-lg text-slate-400 transition-colors"
          >
            <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="pt-3 space-y-2.5 max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
          {/* User-Forged Crates Collapsible Section */}
          {customPacks.length > 0 && (
            <div className="border border-amber-500/30 rounded-xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-950/40 transition-colors">
              <div
                onClick={() => toggleSection('custom')}
                className="flex items-center justify-between p-2.5 bg-amber-500/10 hover:bg-amber-500/15 cursor-pointer select-none transition-colors border-b border-amber-500/20"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  {expandedSections.custom ? (
                    <ChevronDown className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-300 shrink-0" />
                  ) : (
                    <ChevronRight className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <Hammer className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-400 shrink-0" />
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 tracking-wider uppercase truncate">
                    User-Forged Crates
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-amber-400/80 shrink-0">
                    ({customPacks.length})
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {expandedSections.custom && (
                <div className="p-2 space-y-2 bg-black/20 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
                  {customPacks.map(renderCustomPackCard)}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {renderSection(
            'words',
            'Word Expansion Packs',
            wordPacks,
            BookOpen,
            'text-amber-400',
            'text-amber-300'
          )}

          {renderSection(
            'subjects',
            'Subject Cutout Packs',
            subjectPacks,
            Shapes,
            'text-emerald-400',
            'text-emerald-300'
          )}

          {renderSection(
            'backgrounds',
            'Background Texture Packs',
            bgPacks,
            ImageIcon,
            'text-sky-400',
            'text-sky-300'
          )}

          {packs.length === 0 && customPacks.length === 0 && !loading && (
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic text-center py-4">
              No remote or local content packs detected.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* The Crate Forge Modal */}
      <CrateForgeModal
        isOpen={isForgeOpen}
        onClose={() => setIsForgeOpen(false)}
        onSavePack={handleSaveCustomBundle}
        initialPack={editingPackBundle}
      />
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/CustomDropModal.tsx">
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Disc, Sparkles, Upload, Music } from 'lucide-react';
import { CustomDropInput, AlbumEntry } from '../types';

interface CustomDropModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onForge: (input: CustomDropInput) => Promise<void>;
  isLoading: boolean;
}

export const CustomDropModal: React.FC<CustomDropModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onForge,
  isLoading,
}) => {
  const [bandName, setBandName] = useState('');
  const [albumTitle, setAlbumTitle] = useState('');
  const [year, setYear] = useState('');
  const [tracksRaw, setTracksRaw] = useState('');
  const [lore, setLore] = useState('');
  const [review, setReview] = useState('');
  const [audioPreset, setAudioPreset] = useState<AlbumEntry['audioPreset']>('analog-synth');
  const [coverPhoto, setCoverPhoto] = useState<string | null>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (event) => setCoverPhoto(event.target?.result as string);
      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const tracks = tracksRaw
      .split('\n')
      .map((t) => t.trim())
      .filter(Boolean);

    await onForge({
      bandName: bandName || undefined,
      albumTitle: albumTitle || undefined,
      year: year || undefined,
      tracks: tracks.length > 0 ? tracks : undefined,
      lore: lore || undefined,
      review: review || undefined,
      coverImageBase64: coverPhoto || undefined,
      audioPreset,
    });

    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 pt-[calc(0.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-top))] pb-[calc(0.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in select-none">
      <div
        className="w-full max-w-sm sm:max-w-md bg-[#12100e] border border-amber-800/40 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex flex-col max-h-[84dvh] sm:max-h-[88vh] overflow-hidden"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 border-b border-amber-900/40 bg-[#0c0a09] shrink-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Disc className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400"/>
            <h3 className="font-mono font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-200">
              Custom Drop
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Close custom drop"
            className="text-slate-400 hover:text-white p-1 text-sm font-mono leading-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Scrollable Form Body */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-4 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto flex-1 text-xs text-slate-300">
          <div className="p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-950/20 border border-amber-800/30 text-[11px] text-amber-300/90 font-mono leading-relaxed">
            ✦ <strong>Custom Drop:</strong> Enter any fields you choose. Anything left blank will be generated automatically. Custom drops do not consume your 24h Daily Drop timer.
          </div>

          {/* Band Name */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
              Band / Artist Name
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={bandName}
              onChange={(e) => setBandName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g., Unreasonable Elbow (or leave blank)"
              className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Album Title & Year */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
                Album Title
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={albumTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setAlbumTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g., Plastic Spoons"
                className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
                Pressing Year
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={year}
                onChange={(e) => setYear(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g., 1978"
                className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Audio Preset */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
              <Music className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400"/>
              <span>Audio Soundscape Flavor</span>
            </label>
            <select
              value={audioPreset}
              onChange={(e) => setAudioPreset(e.target.value as AlbumEntry['audioPreset'])}
              className="w-full px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono text-xs"
            >
              <option value="analog-synth">1980s Analog Synth & Arpeggio</option>
              <option value="psych-rock">1970s Psychedelic Rock & Phasers</option>
              <option value="krautrock">1970s Krautrock & Motorik Beat</option>
              <option value="post-punk">1980s Angular Post-Punk Riff</option>
              <option value="ambient-drone">Deep Atmospheric Tape Drone</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Custom Artwork */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
              <Upload className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400"/>
              <span>Custom Photo Cover (Optional Image Upload)</span>
            </label>
            <input
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              onChange={handleFileChange}
              className="w-full text-[10px] text-slate-400 file:mr-2 file:py-1 file:px-2.5 file:rounded-md file:border-0 file:text-[10px] file:font-mono file:bg-amber-950/60 file:text-amber-300 hover:file:bg-amber-900 cursor-pointer"
            />
            {coverPhoto && (
              <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-2">
                <img src={coverPhoto} alt="Preview" className="w-8 h-8 rounded border border-amber-600 object-cover" />
                <span className="text-[10px] text-emerald-400 font-mono">Custom photo loaded</span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setCoverPhoto(null)}
                  className="text-[10px] text-rose-400 hover:underline font-mono ml-auto"
                >
                  Remove
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Tracklist */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
              Tracklist (One track per line)
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={3}
              value={tracksRaw}
              onChange={(e) => setTracksRaw(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Side A - First Track&#10;Side A - Second Track&#10;Side B - Finale"
              className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Liner Notes / Band Lore */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
              Liner Notes / Band Bio
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={lore}
              onChange={(e) => setLore(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Recorded in an abandoned cinema using surplus telephone wire..."
              className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Critical Review */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 mb-1">
              Faux Review / Critical Quote
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={review}
              onChange={(e) => setReview(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="An acoustic triumph that defies all conventional protocol."
              className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-black/60 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none font-mono"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Submit Button */}
          <div className="pt-2">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-500 to-amber-600 hover:from-amber-400 text-slate-950 font-mono font-bold uppercase tracking-wider transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-amber-950/40 active:scale-95 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5"/>
              <span>{isLoading ? 'Pressing Custom Drop...' : 'Create Custom Drop'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

<file path="src/services/apparelCanvasEngine.ts">
import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';
import { gagCanvasEngine } from './gagCanvasEngine';

function drawProceduralTextAndStickers(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, album: AlbumEntry) {
  const fontMap: Record<string, string> = {
    medieval: 'MedievalSharp',
    bubble: 'Righteous',
    comic: 'Bangers',
    marker: 'Permanent Marker',
    monospace: 'Space Mono',
    chrome: 'Russo One',
    'vintage-title': 'Russo One',
    'liner-notes': 'Courier Prime',
  };
  const rawFont = fontMap[album.fontFamily || ''] || 'Space Grotesk';

  ctx.save();
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';

  if (album.shadowStyle !== 'none') {
    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.85)';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 25;
    ctx.shadowOffsetX = 12;
    ctx.shadowOffsetY = 12;
  }

  ctx.translate(1800, 1800);
  if (album.skewDeg) {
    ctx.rotate((album.skewDeg * Math.PI) / 180);
  }
  ctx.translate(-1800, -1800);

  let yBand = 1500;
  let yAlbum = 1950;
  const layout = album.titleLayout || '';

  if (layout.includes('top')) {
    yBand = 500;
    yAlbum = 850;
  } else if (layout.includes('bottom')) {
    yBand = 2750;
    yAlbum = 3100;
  } else if (layout.includes('split')) {
    yBand = 500;
    yAlbum = 3100;
  }

  ctx.fillStyle = album.textColor || '#ffffff';
  ctx.font = `900 340px "${rawFont}", sans-serif`;
  ctx.fillText(album.bandName.toUpperCase(), 1800, yBand, 3200);

  ctx.font = `bold italic 220px "${rawFont}", sans-serif`;
  ctx.fillText(`"${album.albumTitle}"`, 1800, yAlbum, 3200);

  if (album.stickers && album.stickers.length > 0) {
    album.stickers.forEach((sticker, idx) => {
      ctx.save();
      const sx = 2900 - idx * 200;
      const sy = 600 + idx * 500;
      ctx.translate(sx, sy);
      ctx.rotate((sticker.rotation * Math.PI) / 180);

      ctx.fillStyle = sticker.bg || '#ef4444';
      ctx.strokeStyle = sticker.border || '#ffffff';
      ctx.lineWidth = 14;

      if (sticker.type === 'circle') {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(0, 0, 320, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.stroke();
      } else {
        ctx.fillRect(-380, -160, 760, 320);
        ctx.strokeRect(-380, -160, 760, 320);
      }

      ctx.fillStyle = sticker.color || '#ffffff';
      ctx.font = '900 65px "Space Grotesk", sans-serif';
      ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
      ctx.fillText(sticker.text, 0, 0, 680);
      ctx.restore();
    });
  }

  ctx.restore();

  ctx.save();
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
  ctx.fillStyle = album.textColor || '#ffffff';
  ctx.globalAlpha = 0.85;
  ctx.font = 'bold 60px "Space Mono", monospace';
  ctx.shadowColor = 'transparent';
  ctx.fillText('Y.D.R.B.N.', 1800, 3560);
  ctx.restore();
}

export function renderHighResFrontGraphic(
  album: AlbumEntry,
  mode: 'cutout' | 'plaque' = 'cutout'
): Promise<string> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = 3600;
    canvas.height = 3600;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    if (!ctx) return resolve(album.coverImageUrl);

    // Use native transparent layer rendering when a procedural recipe exists without a custom photo override
    const sourceUrl =
      mode === 'cutout' && album.recipe && !album.recipe.customPhotoUrl
        ? gagCanvasEngine.renderCover(album.bandName, album.albumTitle, album.recipe, {
            transparentBackdrop: true,
          })
        : album.coverImageUrl;

    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    img.onload = () => {
      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, 3600, 3600);
      drawProceduralTextAndStickers(ctx, album);
      resolve(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
    };
    img.onerror = () => resolve(album.coverImageUrl);
    img.src = sourceUrl;
  });
}
</file>

<file path="src/services/contentPackService.ts">
import { hydrateContentPools } from './proceduralGenerator';
import {
  savePack,
  getInstalledPack,
  removePack,
  StoredPack,
  saveStamp,
  removeStamp,
  savePackRecord,
  getAllInstalledPacks,
} from './packStorage';
import type { CustomPackBundle } from '../components/CrateForgeModal';

export async function getAvailablePacks() {
  const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.endsWith('/') ? import.meta.env.BASE_URL : `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}/`;
  const res = await fetch(`${base}content/index.json`, { cache: 'no-store' });
  const data = await res.json();
  return data.availablePacks;
}

export async function checkPackStatus(packId: string, manifestUrl: string) {
  const local = await getInstalledPack(packId);
  const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.endsWith('/') ? import.meta.env.BASE_URL : `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}/`;
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${base}content/${manifestUrl}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error();
    const remoteManifest = await res.json();
    return {
      isInstalled: !!local,
      installedVersion: local ? local.installedVersion : null,
      remoteVersion: remoteManifest.version,
      hasUpdate: !local || local.installedVersion !== remoteManifest.version,
    };
  } catch {
    return { isInstalled: !!local, installedVersion: local?.installedVersion || null, hasUpdate: false };
  }
}

export async function installCurrentPack(manifestUrl: string): Promise<StoredPack> {
  const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL.endsWith('/') ? import.meta.env.BASE_URL : `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}/`;
  const folderPath = manifestUrl.substring(0, manifestUrl.lastIndexOf('/'));

  const manifestRes = await fetch(`${base}content/${manifestUrl}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
  const manifest = await manifestRes.json();

  let words = {};
  if (manifest.words) {
    const wordsRes = await fetch(`${base}content/${folderPath}/${manifest.words}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
    words = await wordsRes.json();
  }

  // Download background stamps into IndexedDB Blobs
  if (manifest.stamps && Array.isArray(manifest.stamps)) {
    for (const filename of manifest.stamps) {
      const imgRes = await fetch(`${base}content/${folderPath}/stamps/${filename}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
      const blob = await imgRes.blob();
      await saveStamp(filename, blob);
    }
  }

  // Download subject illustrations into IndexedDB Blobs
  if (manifest.subjects && Array.isArray(manifest.subjects)) {
    for (const filename of manifest.subjects) {
      const imgRes = await fetch(`${base}content/${folderPath}/subjects/${filename}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
      const blob = await imgRes.blob();
      await saveStamp(filename, blob);
    }
  }

  await savePack(manifest, words);
  await hydrateContentPools();
  return { id: manifest.id, manifest, words, installedVersion: manifest.version, installedAt: Date.now() };
}

export async function uninstallCurrentPack(packId: string): Promise<void> {
  await removePack(packId);
  await hydrateContentPools();
}

// =========================================================================
// CUSTOM CRATE FORGE SERVICE METHODS
// =========================================================================

/**
 * Converts a data URL to an IndexedDB Blob safely
 */
async function dataUrlToBlob(dataUrl: string): Promise<Blob> {
  if (dataUrl.startsWith('data:')) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(dataUrl);
      return await res.blob();
    } catch {
      // Fallback below
    }
  }
  const parts = dataUrl.split(',');
  const byteString = atob(parts[1] || parts[0]);
  const mimeString = parts[0].split(':')[1]?.split(';')[0] || 'image/png';
  const ab = new ArrayBuffer(byteString.length);
  const ia = new Uint8Array(ab);
  for (let i = 0; i < byteString.length; i++) {
    ia[i] = byteString.charCodeAt(i);
  }
  return new Blob([ab], { type: mimeString });
}

/**
 * Saves a user-authored custom pack bundle into IndexedDB and immediately re-hydrates the randomizer
 */
export async function saveCustomPack(bundle: CustomPackBundle): Promise<StoredPack> {
  const stampKeys: string[] = [];

  // Convert each staged cutout to an IndexedDB Blob
  if (bundle.stamps && Array.isArray(bundle.stamps)) {
    for (const stamp of bundle.stamps) {
      const stampKey = `custom_${stamp.id}.png`;
      stampKeys.push(stampKey);
      const blob = await dataUrlToBlob(stamp.dataUrl);
      await saveStamp(stampKey, blob);
    }
  }

  const manifest = {
    id: bundle.manifest.id,
    name: bundle.manifest.name,
    description: bundle.manifest.description,
    version: bundle.manifest.version,
    author: bundle.manifest.author,
    isCustom: true,
    createdAt: bundle.manifest.createdAt || Date.now(),
    subjects: stampKeys,
    words: 'custom',
  };

  const storedPack: StoredPack = {
    id: bundle.manifest.id,
    manifest,
    words: {
      nouns: bundle.words.nouns || [],
      adjectives: bundle.words.adjectives || [],
      ensembles: bundle.words.ensembles || [],
    },
    installedVersion: bundle.manifest.version,
    installedAt: Date.now(),
    isCustom: true,
    customBundle: bundle,
  };

  await savePackRecord(storedPack);
  await hydrateContentPools();
  return storedPack;
}

/**
 * Returns all custom packs created on this device
 */
export async function getCustomPacks(): Promise<StoredPack[]> {
  const all = await getAllInstalledPacks();
  return all.filter((p) => p.isCustom || p.manifest?.isCustom);
}

/**
 * Uninstalls a custom pack and purges its stored subject stamps from IndexedDB
 */
export async function deleteCustomPack(packId: string): Promise<void> {
  const pack = await getInstalledPack(packId);
  if (pack && pack.manifest?.subjects && Array.isArray(pack.manifest.subjects)) {
    for (const key of pack.manifest.subjects) {
      await removeStamp(key);
    }
  }
  await removePack(packId);
  await hydrateContentPools();
}

/**
 * Downloads a custom pack bundle directly as a standalone .json file
 */
export function exportCustomPackAsJson(bundle: CustomPackBundle): void {
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(bundle, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `${bundle.manifest.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi, '_')}-crate.json`;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
</file>

<file path="src/services/gagCanvasEngine.ts">
/**
 * Modular 3-Layer Visual Gag Canvas Compositor
 * Emulates the dry, humorous, literal visual-gag aesthetic of the classic
 * "Random Band Names Volume One".
 *
 * Decoupled pipeline where every canvas is procedurally assembled from 3 independent layers:
 * 1. Layer 1: Backdrop (8 procedural mathematical routines)
 * 2. Layer 2: Subject Vector (15 core subjects + legacy/starter routines)
 * 3. Layer 3: Gag Modifier (Applied contextually or randomly)
 *
 * CRITICAL: Zero band name or album title text is rendered to the canvas; all typography is handled
