<file path="src/components/VinylSleeve.tsx">
import React from 'react';
import {
  AlbumEntry,
  HypeSticker,
  TitleFontFamily,
  TitleShadowStyle,
  TitleComposition,
} from '../types';
import { Disc, Sparkles, ZoomIn } from 'lucide-react';

interface VinylSleeveProps {
  album: AlbumEntry | null;
  isFlipped: boolean;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isPlayingAudio?: boolean;
  loadingStepText?: string;
  onToggleFlip: () => void;
  onOpenZoom?: () => void;
  hideZoomButton?: boolean;
}

/**
 * Normalizes any TitleComposition enum into one of the 4 guaranteed
 * non-overlapping cover layouts where Band Name and Album Title are separated
 * and pressing/catalog numbers never clutter the front cover.
 */
function normalizeCoverLayout(
  layout?: TitleComposition
): 'top-arc' | 'diagonal-cross' | 'split-corners' | 'bottom-banner' {
  switch (layout) {
    case 'top-arc':
    case 'split-horizontal':
    case 'poster-grid':
      return 'top-arc';
    case 'diagonal-cross':
    case 'center-stack':
    case 'offset-stack':
    case 'overlap-subject':
    case 'giant-background':
      return 'diagonal-cross';
    case 'split-corners':
    case 'corner-stamp':
    case 'floating-label':
    case 'vertical-edge':
      return 'split-corners';
    case 'bottom-banner':
    case 'tiny-caption':
    case 'circular-seal':
    default:
      return 'bottom-banner';
  }
}

export const VinylSleeve: React.FC<VinylSleeveProps> = ({
  album,
  isFlipped,
  isLoading,
  isPlayingAudio = false,
  loadingStepText = 'Pressing Vinyl...',
  onToggleFlip,
  onOpenZoom,
  hideZoomButton = false,
}) => {
  const isFuturePressing = album ? parseInt(album.year, 10) > new Date().getFullYear() : false;

  const tracks = album?.tracks || [
    'Echoes of the Marmalade Sun',
    'Tweed & Turnips (Opus 4)',
    'The Mechanical Goose Walk',
    'Midnight Scone Conspiracy',
    'Biscuits in Minor',
    'Acoustic Affront',
    'The Brass Teapot Lament',
    'Echoes of the Marmalade Sun (Reprise)',
  ];

  const getTrackDuration = (trackName: string, idx: number): string => {
    let h = idx * 17;
    for (let i = 0; i < trackName.length; i++) {
      h = (h * 31 + trackName.charCodeAt(i)) % 997;
    }
    if (h % 29 === 0) return `14:${String(10 + (h % 49)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    if (h % 37 === 0) return `0:${String(12 + (h % 35)).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const mins = 2 + (h % 4);
    const secs = String(h % 60).padStart(2, '0');
    return `${mins}:${secs}`;
  };

  const getBarcodeNumber = (id?: string, year?: string): string => {
    if (!id) return '029778-005';
    let sum = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) sum = (sum * 13 + id.charCodeAt(i)) % 900000;
    return `${String(100000 + sum).slice(0, 6)}-${(year || '1977').slice(-3)}`;
  };

  const getPositionClasses = (pos: HypeSticker['position'], index: number = 0) => {
    switch (pos) {
      case 'top-right':
        return index === 1 ? 'top-10 right-1.5' : 'top-1.5 right-1.5';
      case 'bottom-left':
        return index === 1 ? 'bottom-10 left-1.5' : 'bottom-1.5 left-1.5';
      case 'bottom-right':
        return index === 1 ? 'bottom-10 right-1.5' : 'bottom-1.5 right-1.5';
      case 'top-left':
        return index === 1 ? 'top-10 left-1.5' : 'top-1.5 left-1.5';
      default:
        return 'bottom-1.5 left-1.5';
    }
  };

  const getFontFamilyClass = (fontFamily?: TitleFontFamily): string => {
    switch (fontFamily) {
      case 'medieval':
      case 'ornamental':
        return 'font-medieval';
      case 'bubble':
      case 'circus':
        return 'font-bubble tracking-wider';
      case 'comic':
      case 'graffiti':
        return 'font-comic tracking-widest';
      case 'marker':
      case 'handwritten':
      case 'schoolbook':
        return 'font-marker tracking-wide';
      case 'monospace':
      case 'typewriter':
      case 'arcade':
        return 'font-monospace tracking-normal';
      case 'chrome':
      case 'space':
      case 'futuristic':
        return 'font-chrome tracking-wider';
      case 'stencil':
      case 'industrial':
        return 'font-display tracking-widest';
      case 'western':
      case 'serif':
      case 'newspaper':
      default:
        return 'font-vintage-title tracking-wider';
    }
  };

  const getShadowFilter = (shadowStyle?: TitleShadowStyle): string => {
    switch (shadowStyle) {
      case 'harsh-black':
        return 'drop-shadow(4px 4px 0px #000000) drop-shadow(0 0 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.95))';
      case 'neon-glow':
        return 'drop-shadow(0 0 8px #f59e0b) drop-shadow(0 0 18px rgba(245,158,11,0.65)) drop-shadow(2px 2px 0px #000000)';
      case 'chromatic-3d':
        return 'drop-shadow(-3px 0 0 rgba(239,68,68,0.95)) drop-shadow(3px 0 0 rgba(6,182,212,0.95)) drop-shadow(0 4px 8px #000000)';
      case 'long-shadow':
        return 'drop-shadow(4px 4px 0px rgba(0,0,0,0.95)) drop-shadow(8px 8px 0px rgba(0,0,0,0.8)) drop-shadow(12px 12px 0px rgba(0,0,0,0.65))';
      case 'double-outline':
        return 'drop-shadow(2px 0px 0px #000) drop-shadow(-2px 0px 0px #000) drop-shadow(0px 2px 0px #000) drop-shadow(0px -2px 0px #000) drop-shadow(4px 4px 0px rgba(255,255,255,0.95))';
      case 'offset-print':
        return 'drop-shadow(3px 0px 0px rgba(220,38,38,0.75)) drop-shadow(-3px 0px 0px rgba(6,182,212,0.75))';
      case 'inner-glow':
        return 'drop-shadow(0px 0px 2px #fff) drop-shadow(0px 0px 14px rgba(56,189,248,0.65)) drop-shadow(0px 4px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.85))';
      case 'embossed':
        return 'drop-shadow(-2px -2px 0px rgba(255,255,255,0.75)) drop-shadow(2px 2px 0px rgba(0,0,0,0.85))';
      case 'sticker-outline':
        return 'drop-shadow(2px 0px 0px #fff) drop-shadow(-2px 0px 0px #fff) drop-shadow(0px 2px 0px #fff) drop-shadow(0px -2px 0px #fff) drop-shadow(3px 3px 5px rgba(0,0,0,0.75))';
      case 'electric-outline':
        return 'drop-shadow(0px 0px 2px #fff) drop-shadow(0px 0px 8px rgba(34,211,238,0.95))';
      case 'rubber-stamp':
        return 'drop-shadow(2px 1px 0px rgba(127,29,29,0.85)) drop-shadow(0px 3px 5px rgba(0,0,0,0.45))';
      case 'none':
        return 'drop-shadow(0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,0.65))';
      case 'retro-bevel':
      default:
        return 'drop-shadow(3px 3px 0px #000000) drop-shadow(-1px -1px 0px rgba(255,255,255,0.35)) drop-shadow(0 4px 10px rgba(0,0,0,0.9))';
    }
  };

  const normalizedLayout = normalizeCoverLayout(album?.titleLayout);

  const getAdjustedStickerPosition = (
    layout: 'top-arc' | 'diagonal-cross' | 'split-corners' | 'bottom-banner',
    index: number = 0
  ): HypeSticker['position'] => {
    switch (layout) {
      case 'top-arc':
        // Title is at top; stickers go to bottom corners
        return index === 0 ? 'bottom-left' : 'bottom-right';
      case 'split-corners':
        // Band is top-left, Album is bottom-right; sticker goes strictly to bottom-left
        return 'bottom-left';
      case 'diagonal-cross':
        // Title is across the center; stickers go to opposite outer corners
        return index === 0 ? 'top-right' : 'bottom-left';
      case 'bottom-banner':
      default:
        // Title is locked along bottom; stickers go to top corners
        return index === 0 ? 'top-left' : 'top-right';
    }
  };

  const renderTitleComposition = () => {
    if (!album) return null;

    const fontClass = getFontFamilyClass(album.fontFamily);
    const shadowFilter = getShadowFilter(album.shadowStyle);
    const textColor = album.textColor || '#fef3c7';
    const skew = album.skewDeg || 0;

    const getBandNameSize = (name: string) => {
      if (name.length > 22) return 'text-base sm:text-lg';
      if (name.length > 14) return 'text-lg sm:text-xl';
      return 'text-xl sm:text-2xl';
    };

    switch (normalizedLayout) {
      case 'top-arc': {
        const safeSkew = Math.max(-4, Math.min(4, skew * 0.3));
        const fontSizeClass = getBandNameSize(album.bandName);

        return (
          <div className="absolute top-5 inset-x-4 pointer-events-none flex flex-col items-center justify-start text-center z-10">
            <div
              style={{
                transform: `rotate(${safeSkew}deg)`,
                filter: shadowFilter,
              }}
              className="max-w-[90%] transition-transform duration-500 flex flex-col items-center"
            >
              <h1
                style={{ color: textColor }}
                className={`${fontClass} ${fontSizeClass} font-black uppercase leading-tight pb-0.5 border-b-2 border-amber-400/70 mb-1 text-center break-words`}
              >
                {album.bandName}
              </h1>
              <div className="inline-block bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-400/50 shadow-md mt-0.5">
                <h2 className="font-liner-notes text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-amber-200 uppercase tracking-widest italic leading-none">
                  {album.albumTitle}
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      }

      case 'diagonal-cross': {
        const clampedSkew = Math.max(-12, Math.min(12, skew));
        return (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none flex flex-col justify-center items-center p-6 text-center z-10">
            <div
              style={{
                transform: `rotate(${clampedSkew}deg)`,
                filter: shadowFilter,
              }}
              className="max-w-[78%] transition-transform duration-500 space-y-1.5"
            >
              <h1
                style={{ color: textColor }}
                className={`${fontClass} text-xl sm:text-2xl font-black uppercase leading-none text-center break-words`}
              >
                {album.bandName}
              </h1>
              <div className="inline-block bg-black/65 backdrop-blur-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-amber-400/50">
                <h2 className="font-liner-notes text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-amber-300 uppercase tracking-widest italic">
                  &ldquo;{album.albumTitle}&rdquo;
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      }

      case 'split-corners': {
        const safeCornerSkew = Math.max(-3, Math.min(3, skew));
        return (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none p-4 z-10 flex flex-col justify-between">
            {/* Top-Left: Band Name Only */}
            <div
              style={{
                transform: `rotate(${safeCornerSkew}deg)`,
                filter: shadowFilter,
              }}
              className="max-w-[72%] text-left transition-transform duration-500"
            >
              <h1
                style={{ color: textColor }}
                className={`${fontClass} text-lg sm:text-xl font-black uppercase leading-tight border-l-4 border-amber-400 pl-2 break-words`}
              >
                {album.bandName}
              </h1>
            </div>

            {/* Bottom-Right: Album Title Only */}
            <div
              style={{
                transform: `rotate(${-safeCornerSkew}deg)`,
                filter: shadowFilter,
              }}
              className="max-w-[70%] text-right ml-auto mb-4 transition-transform duration-500"
            >
              <div className="inline-block bg-black/75 backdrop-blur-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded border border-amber-400/60">
                <h2 className="font-liner-notes text-xs sm:text-sm font-bold text-amber-200 uppercase tracking-widest italic">
                  {album.albumTitle}
                </h2>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      }

      case 'bottom-banner':
      default: {
        const safeBannerSkew = Math.max(-2, Math.min(2, skew));
        return (
          <div className="absolute bottom-3 inset-x-3 pointer-events-none z-10">
            <div
              style={{
                transform: `rotate(${safeBannerSkew}deg)`,
                filter: shadowFilter,
              }}
              className="w-full bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-xs border-y-2 border-amber-400 px-3 py-1.5 shadow-2xl transition-transform duration-500 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center"
            >
              <h1
                style={{ color: textColor }}
                className={`${fontClass} text-lg sm:text-xl font-black uppercase leading-tight`}
              >
                {album.bandName}
              </h1>
              <h2 className="font-liner-notes text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-bold text-amber-300 uppercase tracking-widest italic mt-0.5">
                ★ {album.albumTitle} ★
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      }
    }
  };

  // Limit split-corners to at most 1 sticker so bottom-left stays uncluttered
  const visibleStickers = album?.stickers
    ? album.stickers.slice(0, normalizedLayout === 'split-corners' ? 1 : 2)
    : [];

  return (
    <div
      className="relative w-full max-w-[345px] aspect-square mx-auto select-none perspective-1000"
      style={{
        perspective: '1000px',
        WebkitPerspective: '1000px',
      }}
    >
      {/* SLIDING 33⅓ RPM SPINNING VINYL DISC */}
      {album && (
        <div
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={`export-exclude absolute inset-y-2 right-2 aspect-square rounded-full pointer-events-none transition-all duration-700 ease-out z-0 flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_12px_30px_rgba(0,0,0,0.95)] ${
            isPlayingAudio
              ? 'translate-x-[26%] sm:translate-x-[32%] opacity-100'
              : 'translate-x-0 opacity-0'
          }`}
          style={{
            background: isFuturePressing
              ? 'radial-gradient(circle, #06b6d4 0%, #0e7490 35%, #164e63 68%, #083344 100%)'
              : 'radial-gradient(circle, #18181b 0%, #09090b 55%, #18181b 58%, #09090b 75%, #27272a 78%, #09090b 100%)',
          }}
        >
          <div className={`w-full h-full rounded-full flex items-center justify-center relative ${isPlayingAudio ? 'animate-[spin_1.8s_linear_infinite]' : ''}`}>
            <div className="absolute inset-3 rounded-full border border-white/10" />
            <div className="absolute inset-7 rounded-full border border-white/5" />
            <div className="absolute inset-11 rounded-full border border-white/10" />
            <div className="absolute inset-16 rounded-full border border-white/5" />
            <div className="absolute inset-20 rounded-full border border-white/10" />

            <div
              className={`w-28 h-28 rounded-full border-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-between p-2.5 text-center shadow-inner ${
                album.isCustomPressing
                  ? 'bg-[#f8fafc] border-stone-400 text-stone-950'
                  : isFuturePressing
                  ? 'bg-cyan-950 border-cyan-400 text-cyan-200'
                  : 'bg-gradient-to-b from-amber-500 via-amber-600 to-orange-700 border-amber-200/60 text-stone-950'
              }`}
            >
              <span className="font-mono text-[6.5px] font-black tracking-widest uppercase truncate max-w-[75px]">
                {album.isCustomPressing ? 'CUSTOM DROP' : 'Y.D.R.B.N. HI-FI'}
              </span>
              <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full bg-[#12100e] border border-white/30 shadow-inner my-auto" />
              <span className="font-mono text-[6px] font-bold truncate max-w-[82px]">
                {album.bandName.toUpperCase()}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 3D Flip Card Container */}
      <div
        className={`w-full h-full relative transition-transform duration-700 shadow-2xl rounded-md transform-style-3d z-10 ${
          isFlipped ? 'rotate-y-180' : ''
        }`}
        style={{
          transformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
          WebkitTransformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
          transform: isFlipped ? 'rotateY(180deg)' : 'rotateY(0deg)',
          WebkitTransform: isFlipped ? 'rotateY(180deg)' : 'rotateY(0deg)',
        }}
      >
        {/* ==================== FRONT SLEEVE ==================== */}
        <div
          id="sleeve-front"
          onClick={() => {
            if (!isLoading && album) onToggleFlip();
          }}
          className="sleeve-front absolute inset-0 w-full h-full rounded-md overflow-hidden bg-stone-900 border-2 border-stone-300/40 ring-1 ring-black/90 shadow-[0_16px_36px_rgba(0,0,0,0.95)] cursor-pointer group backface-hidden"
          style={{
            backfaceVisibility: 'hidden',
            WebkitBackfaceVisibility: 'hidden',
            transform: 'rotateY(0deg)',
            WebkitTransform: 'rotateY(0deg)',
            transformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
            WebkitTransformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
          }}
        >
          {album?.coverImageUrl ? (
            <img
              src={album.coverImageUrl}
              alt={`${album.bandName} - ${album.albumTitle}`}
              className="w-full h-full object-cover select-none pointer-events-none filter contrast-[1.05] brightness-95"
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center bg-gradient-to-b from-[#181a24] to-[#0f1118] relative">
              <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-20 pointer-events-none flex items-center justify-center">
                <Disc className="w-72 h-72 text-orange-500 animate-[spin_40s_linear_infinite]"/>
              </div>
              <div className="relative z-10 space-y-2">
                <div className="w-14 h-14 mx-auto rounded-full bg-orange-500/10 border border-orange-500/30 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Sparkles className="w-7 h-7 text-orange-400"/>
                </div>
                <h3 className="font-vintage-title text-base font-bold text-amber-200 uppercase tracking-widest">
                  Master Tape Ready
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-[200px] leading-relaxed">
                  Hit <span className="text-orange-400 font-bold">⚡ Daily Drop</span> to forge today&apos;s surreal fictional band and vinyl artwork!
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Pinched Cardboard Left Spine Crease */}
          <div className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-black/65 via-white/15 to-black/35 pointer-events-none z-20" />

          {/* Analog Vinyl Ring-Wear Overlay */}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 vinyl-ring-wear pointer-events-none opacity-20" />

          {/* Vintage Sleeve Edge Crease and Vignette */}
          <div className="absolute inset-0 shadow-[inset_0_0_24px_rgba(0,0,0,0.85)] pointer-events-none border border-white/5" />

          {/* Temporal Anomaly: Holographic Foil, Lenticular Ridges & Chrono-Sash */}
          {isFuturePressing && (
            <>
              <div className="absolute inset-0 holographic-foil pointer-events-none z-20" />
              <div className="absolute inset-0 lenticular-ridges pointer-events-none z-20 opacity-80" />

              <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 z-30 pointer-events-none flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-black/75 border border-cyan-400/60 rounded px-2 py-0.5 shadow-[0_0_12px_rgba(6,182,212,0.45)] backdrop-blur-xs">
                <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3 text-cyan-300 animate-spin"/>
                <span className="font-mono text-[9px] font-black tracking-wider text-cyan-200 uppercase">
                  {album?.year} FUTURE PRESSING
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center pointer-events-none overflow-hidden select-none z-20">
                <div className="w-[140%] py-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-cyan-950/85 to-transparent border-y border-cyan-400/40 backdrop-blur-[1px] rotate-[-22deg] flex flex-col items-center justify-center shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(6,182,212,0.25)]">
                  <span className="font-mono text-[10px] sm:text-xs font-black tracking-[0.35em] uppercase text-cyan-100 drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(34,211,238,0.7)] whitespace-nowrap">
                    ✦ TEMPORAL ANOMALY • FROM THE FUTURE ✦
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-[8px] tracking-[0.2em] uppercase text-cyan-300/80 font-semibold">
                    AUTHENTIC {album?.year} MASTER TAPE
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          {/* Dynamic Multi-Font Overlay */}
          {renderTitleComposition()}

          {/* Authentic Hype Stickers (With Giant Override for Elbow Failsafe Mode) */}
          {visibleStickers.map((st, idx) => {
            const isFailsafeSticker = st.id.startsWith('stk-elbow-failsafe');
            const adjustedPos = getAdjustedStickerPosition(normalizedLayout, idx);
            return (
              <div
                key={st.id}
                className={`absolute ${getPositionClasses(adjustedPos, idx)} ${
                  isFailsafeSticker ? 'z-30' : 'z-20'
                } pointer-events-none transition-transform group-hover:scale-105`}
                style={{
                  transform: `rotate(${st.rotation}deg)`,
                }}
              >
                <div
                  style={{
                    backgroundColor: st.bg,
                    color: st.color,
                    borderColor: st.border,
                    width: 'fit-content',
                    padding: isFailsafeSticker ? '6px 10px' : '3px 7px',
                  }}
                  className={
                    isFailsafeSticker
                      ? 'text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-mono font-black tracking-wider uppercase shadow-[0_10px_25px_rgba(0,0,0,0.95)] border-[3px] ring-2 ring-black max-w-[165px] sm:max-w-[185px] text-center leading-snug rounded-md'
                      : 'text-[8px] font-black tracking-tight uppercase shadow-md border-2 max-w-[76px] text-center leading-tight rounded-sm'
                  }
                >
                  {st.text}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}

          {/* Subtle Zoom & Flip Badges */}
          {album && (
            <div className="export-exclude absolute bottom-1 right-1 z-30 flex items-center gap-1 opacity-25 hover:opacity-100 active:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300">
              {onOpenZoom && !hideZoomButton && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={(e) => {
                    e.stopPropagation();
                    onOpenZoom();
                  }}
                  className="bg-black/75 hover:bg-black/90 text-amber-200/90 hover:text-amber-100 text-[9px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-white/15 flex items-center gap-1 shadow-md transition-all active:scale-90 pointer-events-auto"
                  title="Zoom Artwork"
                >
                  <ZoomIn className="w-2.5 h-2.5 text-amber-400"/>
                  <span>Zoom</span>
                </button>
              )}
              <div className="bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs text-amber-200/70 text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-mono border border-white/10 opacity-70 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity pointer-events-none">
                ↺ Flip
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ==================== FLIP VIEW: BACK SLEEVE & TRACKS ==================== */}
        <div
          id="sleeve-back"
          onClick={() => {
            if (!isLoading) onToggleFlip();
          }}
          className="sleeve-back absolute inset-0 w-full h-full rounded-md overflow-hidden bg-stone-900 border-2 border-stone-300/40 ring-1 ring-black/90 shadow-2xl p-2.5 sm:p-3 flex flex-col justify-between text-stone-200 cursor-pointer select-none backface-hidden rotate-y-180"
          style={{
            backfaceVisibility: 'hidden',
            WebkitBackfaceVisibility: 'hidden',
            transform: 'rotateY(180deg)',
            WebkitTransform: 'rotateY(180deg)',
            transformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
            WebkitTransformStyle: 'preserve-3d',
          }}
        >
          {album?.coverImageUrl && (
            <img
              src={album.coverImageUrl}
              className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full object-cover select-none pointer-events-none scale-x-[-1] brightness-[0.65]"
              alt="Back Cover Texture"
            />
          )}

          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/25 backdrop-blur-[12px] pointer-events-none" />
          <div className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 w-2.5 bg-gradient-to-l from-black/65 via-white/15 to-black/35 pointer-events-none z-20" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 vinyl-ring-wear pointer-events-none opacity-20 mix-blend-overlay" />
          <div className="absolute inset-0 shadow-[inset_0_0_40px_rgba(0,0,0,0.7)] pointer-events-none mix-blend-multiply" />

          {/* 1. Header: Band name, album title, release year, catalog number */}
          <div className="relative z-10 shrink-0 border-b border-amber-500/30 pb-1 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <h2 className="font-vintage-title text-[11px] sm:text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300 leading-tight truncate">
                {album?.bandName || 'THE SURREAL APOSTLES'}
              </h2>
              <h3 className="font-liner-notes text-[10px] sm:text-[11px] font-bold text-stone-300 uppercase tracking-tight italic leading-tight truncate">
                {album?.albumTitle || 'Acoustic Aberrations'}
              </h3>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right shrink-0">
              <span className="font-mono text-[8.5px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-orange-400 bg-orange-950/60 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-orange-500/30">
                {album?.catalogNumber || 'YDR-1977-STEREO'}
              </span>
              <p className="font-mono text-[7.5px] sm:text-[8px] text-stone-400 mt-0.5">
                © {album?.year || '1977'} RECORD CORP
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* 2. Tracklist */}
          <div className="relative z-10 shrink-0 bg-black/45 rounded p-1.5 sm:p-2 border border-stone-800/90 my-1 text-[8.5px] sm:text-[9px] font-liner-notes">
            <div className="font-bold text-[8.5px] sm:text-[9px] text-amber-400/90 border-b border-stone-700/80 pb-0.5 mb-1 uppercase tracking-wider flex justify-between items-center">
              <span>Program Tracklist</span>
              <span className="text-stone-500 text-[7.5px] sm:text-[8px] font-mono">33⅓ RPM • FULL STEREO</span>
            </div>
            <ul className="space-y-[1px] text-stone-300">
              {tracks.map((track, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 leading-none">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="text-orange-400/90 font-mono text-[8px] sm:text-[8.5px] font-bold shrink-0">
                      {String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}.
                    </span>
                    <span className="truncate leading-tight text-stone-200">
                      {track}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <span className="text-[7.5px] sm:text-[8px] text-stone-500 font-mono shrink-0 pl-1">
                    {getTrackDuration(track, i)}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </div>

          {/* 3. Faux Reviews & Band Lore */}
          <div className="relative z-10 flex-1 min-h-0 bg-black/45 rounded p-1.5 sm:p-2 border border-stone-800/80 my-0.5 flex flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden">
            {album?.fauxReviews && album.fauxReviews.length > 0 && (
              <div className="text-[8px] sm:text-[8.5px] font-liner-notes italic text-amber-200/90 shrink-0 line-clamp-2 leading-tight">
                • {album.fauxReviews[0]}
              </div>
            )}

            {album?.bandBio && (
              <p className="text-[7.5px] sm:text-[8px] text-stone-400 leading-tight font-liner-notes border-t border-stone-800/80 pt-1 overflow-hidden line-clamp-3 sm:line-clamp-4">
                {album.bandBio}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* 4. Barcode & Hi-Fi Footer */}
          <div className="relative z-10 shrink-0 pt-1 border-t border-stone-800 flex items-center justify-between text-[8px] font-mono text-stone-500">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="px-1 py-0.2 border border-stone-600 text-stone-300 font-black tracking-widest text-[7px] rounded-xs">
                HI-FI
              </span>
              <span className="px-1 py-0.2 border border-amber-600/60 text-amber-400 font-bold text-[7px] rounded-xs">
                FULL STEREO
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="text-[7px] text-stone-500 text-center truncate max-w-[140px]">
              Y.D.R.B.N. MASTER SOUND LABS • ALL RIGHTS RESERVED
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col items-center bg-white px-1 py-0.5 rounded-xs">
              <div className="flex gap-[1px] h-3 items-end">
                <div className="w-[1.5px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[1px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[2px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[1px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[3px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[1px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[2px] h-full bg-black" />
                <div className="w-[1px] h-full bg-black" />
              </div>
              <span className="text-[6px] text-black font-mono leading-none tracking-tighter">
                {getBarcodeNumber(album?.id, album?.year)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Loading Spinner Overlay */}
      {isLoading && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 z-40 bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-xs rounded-md flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-6 text-center shadow-2xl border border-orange-500/30">
          <div className="relative w-16 h-16 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
            <Disc className="w-16 h-16 text-orange-500 animate-[spin_2s_linear_infinite]"/>
            <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
              <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-stone-900 border-2 border-orange-400" />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="font-vintage-title text-sm font-bold text-amber-200 uppercase tracking-widest">
            {loadingStepText}
          </p>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1 font-mono max-w-[200px] animate-pulse">
            Rendering Visual Gag on Canvas...
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/services/assetManager.ts">
import { getStamp } from './packStorage';

export const imageCache: Record<string, HTMLImageElement> = {};

// Helper: True only if the identifier is a real file resource, not a procedural canvas routine
function isFetchableImage(filename: string): boolean {
  return (
    /\.(png|jpe?g|svg|webp|gif|avif)$/i.test(filename) ||
    filename.startsWith('data:') ||
    filename.startsWith('blob:')
  );
}

export async function loadAsset(filename?: string): Promise<HTMLImageElement | null> {
  if (!filename) return null;

  const cleanName = filename.split('/').pop() || filename;
  if (imageCache[cleanName]) return imageCache[cleanName];

  return new Promise(async (resolve) => {
    // 1. Check IndexedDB first (stamps from installed cargo packs)
    try {
      const blob = await getStamp(cleanName);
      if (blob) {
        const img = new Image();
        img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
        img.src = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        img.onload = () => {
          if (img.naturalWidth === 0) { resolve(null); return; }
          imageCache[cleanName] = img;
          resolve(img);
        };
        img.onerror = () => resolve(null);
        return;
      }
    } catch {
      // Proceed to fallback
    }

    // 2. If it is a procedural canvas routine (no image file extension), do not fire a network request
    if (!isFetchableImage(cleanName)) {
      resolve(null);
      return;
    }

    // 3. Static image file fallback (/assets/)
    const img = new Image();
    img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    img.src = `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/${cleanName}`;

    img.onload = () => {
      if (img.naturalWidth === 0) { resolve(null); return; }
      imageCache[cleanName] = img;
      resolve(img);
    };
    img.onerror = () => {
      resolve(null);
    };
  });
}

export async function loadRecipeAssets(
  backdrop?: string,
  subject?: string
): Promise<void> {
  const tasks: Promise<HTMLImageElement | null>[] = [];
  if (backdrop) tasks.push(loadAsset(backdrop));
  if (subject) tasks.push(loadAsset(subject));
  await Promise.all(tasks);
}

export async function preloadCustomAssets(): Promise<void[]> {
  return Promise.resolve([]);
}

export function getOrLoadImage(filename: string): HTMLImageElement | undefined {
  if (!filename) return undefined;
  const cleanName = filename.split('/').pop() || filename;
  if (imageCache[cleanName]) return imageCache[cleanName];

  // Skip procedural names without image extensions
  if (!isFetchableImage(cleanName)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const img = new Image();
  img.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
  img.src = `${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}assets/${cleanName}`;
  img.onload = () => { imageCache[cleanName] = img; };
  imageCache[cleanName] = img;
  return img;
}
</file>

<file path="src/services/audioSynthesizer.ts">
/**
 * Web Audio API Algorithmic Synthesizer Engine with Hybrid Stem Mixing
 * Generates multi-instrument retro music clips, rhythmic arpeggios,
 * analog percussion, and layered vintage stem recordings.
 */

import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';
import { getOrSelectStemsForAlbum, loadStemBuffer } from './stemAudioService';

export class RetroAudioSynthesizer {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private isPlaying = false;
  private activeNodes: { stop: () => void }[] = [];
  private activeTimeouts: NodeJS.Timeout[] = [];
  private analyser: AnalyserNode | null = null;
  private freqData: Uint8Array<ArrayBuffer> | null = null;

  private getContext(): AudioContext {
    if (!this.ctx) {
      const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext;
      this.ctx = new AudioCtx();
    }
    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      this.ctx.resume();
    }
    return this.ctx;
  }

  public stopAll(): void {
    this.activeTimeouts.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
    this.activeTimeouts = [];

    this.activeNodes.forEach((node) => {
      try {
        node.stop();
      } catch {
        // node already stopped
      }
    });
    this.activeNodes = [];
    this.isPlaying = false;
  }

  public async playAlbumClip(
    albumId: string,
    albumTitle: string,
    yearStr: string = '1975',
    preset?: AlbumEntry['audioPreset']
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    this.stopAll();
    const ctx = this.getContext();
    this.isPlaying = true;

    const parsedYear = parseInt(yearStr.replace(/\D/g, ''), 10) || 1977;

    // 1. Resolve or retrieve the permanent stem plan for this specific album drop
    const plan = getOrSelectStemsForAlbum(albumId, yearStr);

    if (plan.isAtjFanfare) {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ydrbn-atj-fanfare'));
    }

    // 2. Pre-load audio buffers to determine exact clip length
    const loadedBuffers = await Promise.all(
      plan.stems.map((filename) => loadStemBuffer(filename, ctx))
    );

    // Cancel if stopAll() was called while buffers were loading
    if (!this.isPlaying) return false;

    const validBuffers = loadedBuffers.filter((b): b is AudioBuffer => b !== null);

    let maxDuration = 6.2;
    if (validBuffers.length > 0) {
      const stemMax = Math.max(...validBuffers.map((b) => b.duration));
      maxDuration = Math.max(stemMax, 5.0);
    }
    const duration = maxDuration;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    // 3. Master Output Bus with analog compressor
    const masterComp = ctx.createDynamicsCompressor();
    masterComp.threshold.setValueAtTime(-18, now);
    masterComp.knee.setValueAtTime(12, now);
    masterComp.ratio.setValueAtTime(4, now);
    masterComp.attack.setValueAtTime(0.005, now);
    masterComp.release.setValueAtTime(0.15, now);

    const masterGain = ctx.createGain();
    masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, now);
    masterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.75, now + 0.3);
    masterGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.75, now + duration - 0.5);
    masterGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + duration);
    masterComp.connect(masterGain);
    masterGain.connect(ctx.destination);

    // 2. Inside playAlbumClip(), right after masterGain.connect(ctx.destination):
    this.analyser = ctx.createAnalyser();
    this.analyser.fftSize = 64;
    this.analyser.smoothingTimeConstant = 0.72;
    this.freqData = new Uint8Array(this.analyser.frequencyBinCount);
    masterGain.connect(this.analyser);

    // 4. Play Continuous needle-drop vinyl crackle
    this.playVinylCrackle(ctx, masterComp, now, duration);

    // 5. Trigger Sample Stems through master compression
    if (validBuffers.length > 0) {
      const stemVol = plan.isSoloHarmonica || plan.isAtjFanfare ? 0.95 : 0.8 / Math.sqrt(validBuffers.length);
      validBuffers.forEach((buffer) => {
        const source = ctx.createBufferSource();
        source.buffer = buffer;

        const stemGain = ctx.createGain();
        stemGain.gain.setValueAtTime(stemVol, now);

        source.connect(stemGain);
        stemGain.connect(masterComp);

        source.start(now);
        this.activeNodes.push(source);
      });
    }

    // 6. Play Procedural Synth attenuated to 25% volume
    const shouldPlaySynth = plan.enableSynthBacking || validBuffers.length === 0;

    if (shouldPlaySynth && !plan.isSoloHarmonica && !plan.isAtjFanfare) {
      // Sub-bus for 25% synth level
      const synthSubGain = ctx.createGain();
      synthSubGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, now);
      synthSubGain.connect(masterComp);

      let hash = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < albumTitle.length; i++) {
        hash = (hash * 31 + albumTitle.charCodeAt(i)) % 100000;
      }
      const baseRoots = [110.0, 130.81, 146.83, 164.81, 174.61, 196.0];
      const root = baseRoots[hash % baseRoots.length];

      // Rotate synth presets dynamically for modern/future releases (>=1990)
      const modernPresets: AlbumEntry['audioPreset'][] = [
        'analog-synth',
        'post-punk',
        'krautrock',
        'ambient-drone',
      ];

      const activePreset: AlbumEntry['audioPreset'] = preset || (
        parsedYear < 1974
          ? 'psych-rock'
          : parsedYear <= 1978
          ? 'krautrock'
          : parsedYear <= 1983
          ? 'analog-synth'
          : parsedYear <= 1989
          ? 'post-punk'
          : modernPresets[hash % modernPresets.length]
      );

      switch (activePreset) {
        case 'analog-synth':
          this.playAnalogSynthRoutine(ctx, synthSubGain, now, duration, root, hash);
          break;
        case 'krautrock':
          this.playKrautrockRoutine(ctx, synthSubGain, now, duration, root, hash);
          break;
        case 'post-punk':
          this.playPostPunkRoutine(ctx, synthSubGain, now, duration, root, hash);
          break;
        case 'psych-rock':
          this.playPsychRockRoutine(ctx, synthSubGain, now, duration, root, hash);
          break;
        case 'ambient-drone':
        default:
          this.playAmbientDroneRoutine(ctx, synthSubGain, now, duration, root, hash);
          break;
      }
    }

    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        this.isPlaying = false;
        resolve(true);
      }, duration * 1000);
      this.activeTimeouts.push(timeout);
    });
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // SYNTHESIS ROUTINES
  // =========================================================================
  private playAnalogSynthRoutine(
    ctx: AudioContext,
    out: AudioNode,
    start: number,
    duration: number,
    root: number,
    hash: number
  ) {
    const semitones = [0, 3, 5, 7, 10, 12, 15, 12, 7, 10, 5, 3];
    const stepTime = 0.16;
    const totalSteps = Math.floor((duration - 0.4) / stepTime);

    for (let i = 0; i < totalSteps; i++) {
      const noteStart = start + i * stepTime;
      const semi = semitones[(i + (hash % 4)) % semitones.length];
      const freq = root * 2 * Math.pow(2, semi / 12);

      const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = 'lowpass';
      filter.Q.setValueAtTime(6.5, noteStart);
      filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(3200, noteStart);
      filter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(350, noteStart + stepTime * 0.95);

      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, noteStart);
      gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.24, noteStart + 0.015);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, noteStart + stepTime * 0.9);

      [-5, 5].forEach((cents) => {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        osc.type = 'sawtooth';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, noteStart);
        osc.detune.setValueAtTime(cents, noteStart);

        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(out);

        osc.start(noteStart);
        osc.stop(noteStart + stepTime);
        this.activeNodes.push(osc);
      });

      if (i % 4 === 0) {
        this.triggerKick(ctx, out, noteStart, 0.28);
      }
    }
  }

  private playKrautrockRoutine(
    ctx: AudioContext,
    out: AudioNode,
    start: number,
    duration: number,
    root: number,
    hash: number
  ) {
    const stepTime = 0.24;
    const totalSteps = Math.floor((duration - 0.4) / stepTime);
    const bassScale = [0, 0, 7, 0, 5, 0, 3, 5];

    for (let i = 0; i < totalSteps; i++) {
      const stepStart = start + i * stepTime;
      const semi = bassScale[(i + (hash % 2)) % bassScale.length];
      const freq = root * Math.pow(2, semi / 12);

      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      osc.type = 'sawtooth';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, stepStart);

      const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      filter.type = 'lowpass';
      filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, stepStart);
      filter.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(280, stepStart + stepTime * 0.9);
      filter.Q.setValueAtTime(4.0, stepStart);

      const gain = ctx.createGain();
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, stepStart);
      gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.3, stepStart + 0.02);
      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, stepStart + stepTime * 0.85);

      osc.connect(filter);
      filter.connect(gain);
      gain.connect(out);

      osc.start(stepStart);
      osc.stop(stepStart + stepTime);
      this.activeNodes.push(osc);

      if (i % 2 === 0) {
        this.triggerKick(ctx, out, stepStart, 0.32);
      } else {
        this.triggerSnare(ctx, out, stepStart, 0.18);
      }
      this.triggerHiHat(ctx, out, stepStart + stepTime * 0.5, 0.08);
    }
  }

  private playPostPunkRoutine(
    ctx: AudioContext,
    out: AudioNode,
    start: number,
    duration: number,
    root: number,
    hash: number
  ) {
    const stepTime = 0.22;
    const totalSteps = Math.floor((duration - 0.4) / stepTime);
    const riff = [0, 1, 3, 5, 7, 5, 3, 1, 0, 7, 5, 3];

    for (let i = 0; i < totalSteps; i++) {
      const stepStart = start + i * stepTime;
      const semi = riff[i % riff.length];
      const freq = root * Math.pow(2, semi / 12);

      [-8, 0, 8].forEach((detune, idx) => {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        osc.type = idx === 1 ? 'square' : 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, stepStart);
        osc.detune.setValueAtTime(detune, stepStart);

        const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        filter.type = 'bandpass';
        filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(1400, stepStart);
        filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1.8, stepStart);

        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, stepStart);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.18, stepStart + 0.02);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, stepStart + stepTime * 0.9);

        osc.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(out);

        osc.start(stepStart);
        osc.stop(stepStart + stepTime);
        this.activeNodes.push(osc);
      });

      if (i % 4 === 0) {
        this.triggerKick(ctx, out, stepStart, 0.3);
      }
      if (i % 4 === 2) {
        this.triggerSnare(ctx, out, stepStart, 0.22);
      }
    }
  }

  private playPsychRockRoutine(
    ctx: AudioContext,
    out: AudioNode,
    start: number,
    duration: number,
    root: number,
    hash: number
  ) {
    const chordNotes = [root, root * 1.2, root * 1.5, root * 2.0];
    const lfo = ctx.createOscillator();
    lfo.frequency.setValueAtTime(4.8, start);
    const lfoGain = ctx.createGain();
    lfoGain.gain.setValueAtTime(9.0, start);

    chordNotes.forEach((freq, idx) => {
      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
      osc.type = idx % 2 === 0 ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
      lfo.connect(osc.frequency);

      const noteGain = ctx.createGain();
      noteGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, start);
      noteGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.14, start + 0.8);
      noteGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.14, start + duration - 0.8);
      noteGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + duration);

      osc.connect(noteGain);
      noteGain.connect(out);

      osc.start(start);
      osc.stop(start + duration);
      this.activeNodes.push(osc);
    });

    lfo.start(start);
    lfo.stop(start + duration);
    this.activeNodes.push(lfo);

    const leadSteps = [12, 15, 17, 19, 17, 15, 12, 7];
    const stepTime = 0.36;
    const totalSteps = Math.floor((duration - 1.2) / stepTime);

    for (let s = 0; s < totalSteps; s++) {
      const noteStart = start + 0.6 + s * stepTime;
      const semi = leadSteps[(s + (hash % 3)) % leadSteps.length];
      const freq = root * Math.pow(2, semi / 12);

      const leadOsc = ctx.createOscillator();
      leadOsc.type = 'sawtooth';
      leadOsc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, noteStart);

      const leadFilter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
      leadFilter.type = 'lowpass';
      leadFilter.frequency.setValueAtTime(2200, noteStart);
      leadFilter.Q.setValueAtTime(3.0, noteStart);

      const leadGain = ctx.createGain();
      leadGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, noteStart);
      leadGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.18, noteStart + 0.03);
      leadGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, noteStart + stepTime * 0.9);

      leadOsc.connect(leadFilter);
      leadFilter.connect(leadGain);
      leadGain.connect(out);

      leadOsc.start(noteStart);
      leadOsc.stop(noteStart + stepTime);
      this.activeNodes.push(leadOsc);
    }
  }

  private playAmbientDroneRoutine(
    ctx: AudioContext,
    out: AudioNode,
    start: number,
    duration: number,
    root: number,
    hash: number
  ) {
    const padNotes = [root * 0.5, root, root * 1.5, root * 2.25];

    padNotes.forEach((freq, idx) => {
      [-1.5, 1.5].forEach((detune) => {
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        osc.type = idx === 0 ? 'sine' : 'triangle';
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
        osc.detune.setValueAtTime(detune, start);

        const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
        filter.type = 'lowpass';
        filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(350, start);
        filter.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(1400, start + duration * 0.5);
        filter.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(400, start + duration);

        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, start);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.16, start + 1.5);
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.16, start + duration - 1.5);
        gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + duration);

        osc.connect(filter);
        filter.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(out);

        osc.start(start);
        osc.stop(start + duration);
        this.activeNodes.push(osc);
      });
    });

    // High crystal bell harmonics scattered throughout
    const bellIntervals = [19, 24, 26, 31];
    bellIntervals.forEach((semi, i) => {
      const bellTime = start + 0.8 + i * 1.1;
      const freq = root * Math.pow(2, semi / 12);

      const bell = ctx.createOscillator();
      bell.type = 'sine';
      bell.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, bellTime);

      const bellGain = ctx.createGain();
      bellGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, bellTime);
      bellGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.08, bellTime + 0.02);
      bellGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, bellTime + 1.8);

      bell.connect(bellGain);
      bellGain.connect(out);

      bell.start(bellTime);
      bell.stop(bellTime + 1.9);
      this.activeNodes.push(bell);
    });
  } // <-- Closing brace for playAmbientDroneRoutine restored!

  // =========================================================================
  // ANALOG PERCUSSION & VINYL CRACKLE
  // =========================================================================
  private triggerKick(ctx: AudioContext, out: AudioNode, time: number, vol: number) {
    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = ctx.createGain();

    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(140, time);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(36, time + 0.12);

    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, time);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.14);

    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(out);

    osc.start(time);
    osc.stop(time + 0.15);
    this.activeNodes.push(osc);
  }

  private triggerSnare(ctx: AudioContext, out: AudioNode, time: number, vol: number) {
    const bufferSize = ctx.sampleRate * 0.12;
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
      data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    }

    const noise = ctx.createBufferSource();
    noise.buffer = buffer;

    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    filter.type = 'bandpass';
    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(1600, time);
    filter.Q.setValueAtTime(1.2, time);

    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, time);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.11);

    noise.connect(filter);
    filter.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(out);

    noise.start(time);
    noise.stop(time + 0.12);
    this.activeNodes.push(noise);

    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
    const thudGain = ctx.createGain();
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(180, time);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(80, time + 0.08);

    thudGain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * 0.5, time);
    thudGain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.09);

    osc.connect(thudGain);
    thudGain.connect(out);

    osc.start(time);
    osc.stop(time + 0.1);
    this.activeNodes.push(osc);
  }

  private triggerHiHat(ctx: AudioContext, out: AudioNode, time: number, vol: number) {
    const bufferSize = ctx.sampleRate * 0.04;
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
      data[i] = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
    }

    const noise = ctx.createBufferSource();
    noise.buffer = buffer;

    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    filter.type = 'highpass';
    filter.frequency.setValueAtTime(7000, time);

    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, time);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time + 0.035);

    noise.connect(filter);
    filter.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(out);

    noise.start(time);
    noise.stop(time + 0.04);
    this.activeNodes.push(noise);
  }

  private playVinylCrackle(ctx: AudioContext, out: AudioNode, start: number, duration: number) {
    const bufferSize = ctx.sampleRate * duration;
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, bufferSize, ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);

    let b0 = 0, b1 = 0, b2 = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < bufferSize; i++) {
      const white = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
      b0 = 0.99886 * b0 + white * 0.0555179;
      b1 = 0.99332 * b1 + white * 0.0750759;
      b2 = 0.969 * b2 + white * 0.153852;
      let sample = (b0 + b1 + b2) * 0.025;

      if (Math.random() < 0.0007) {
        sample += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.35;
      }
      data[i] = sample;
    }

    const noise = ctx.createBufferSource();
    noise.buffer = buffer;

    const filter = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    filter.type = 'bandpass';
    filter.frequency.value = 1900;
    filter.Q.value = 0.7;

    const gain = ctx.createGain();
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.001, start);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.06, start + 0.4);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.06, start + duration - 0.6);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.001, start + duration);

    noise.connect(filter);
    filter.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(out);

    noise.start(start);
    noise.stop(start + duration);
    this.activeNodes.push(noise);
  }

  public getPlaybackStatus(): boolean {
    return this.isPlaying;
  }

  // 3. Add this method to RetroAudioSynthesizer class:
  public getAudioLevels(): { left: number; right: number } {
    if (!this.isPlaying || !this.analyser || !this.freqData) {
      return { left: 0, right: 0 };
    }
    this.analyser.getByteFrequencyData(this.freqData);
    let lowSum = 0;
    let highSum = 0;
    const half = Math.floor(this.freqData.length / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < half; i++) lowSum += this.freqData[i];
    for (let i = half; i < this.freqData.length; i++) highSum += this.freqData[i];

    const left = Math.min(1, (lowSum / (half * 200)) * 1.25);
    const right = Math.min(1, (highSum / (half * 160)) * 1.45);
    return { left, right };
  }
}

export const audioSynthesizer = new RetroAudioSynthesizer();
</file>

<file path="src/services/shareImageService.ts">
import { toBlob } from 'html-to-image';
import { AlbumEntry } from '../types';

/**
 * Filter out in-app UI chrome (like "Zoom" and "↺ Flip" badges)
 * from being included in the exported share image.
 */
const filterUiChrome = (node: HTMLElement) => {
  if (node.classList && node.classList.contains('export-exclude')) {
    return false;
  }
  return true;
};

// 1. Capture WYSIWYG Front Cover (Rendered at 3x resolution ~1020x1020)
export async function compositeFrontSleeve(album: AlbumEntry): Promise<Blob> {
  const frontEl = document.getElementById('sleeve-front');
  if (!frontEl) {
    throw new Error('Front sleeve element not found in DOM');
  }

  // Ensure all Google fonts and web fonts are fully rendered
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.fonts) {
    await document.fonts.ready.catch(() => {});
  }

  const blob = await toBlob(frontEl, {
    quality: 0.95,
    pixelRatio: 3, // Scales 340px to ~1020px for crisp high-resolution output
    filter: filterUiChrome,
  });

  if (!blob) {
    throw new Error('Failed to generate front sleeve image blob');
  }

  return blob;
}

// 2. Capture WYSIWYG Back Sleeve (Un-rotates the 3D CSS flip for a flat image)
export async function compositeBackSleeve(album: AlbumEntry): Promise<Blob> {
  const backEl = document.getElementById('sleeve-back');
  if (!backEl) {
    throw new Error('Back sleeve element not found in DOM');
  }

  if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.fonts) {
    await document.fonts.ready.catch(() => {});
  }

  const blob = await toBlob(backEl, {
    quality: 0.95,
    pixelRatio: 3,
    filter: filterUiChrome,
    // Temporarily un-rotate the 3D card flip so the export is upright
    style: {
      transform: 'none',
      WebkitTransform: 'none',
      backfaceVisibility: 'visible',
      WebkitBackfaceVisibility: 'visible',
    },
  });

  if (!blob) {
    throw new Error('Failed to generate back sleeve image blob');
  }

  return blob;
}

import {

  HelpCircle,

  Sparkles,
  Archive,
  AlertCircle,
  ChevronDown,

export const HelpModal: React.FC<HelpModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {

                >
                  <span className="text-amber-400">▶</span>
                  <span>Watch Functionality Walkthrough</span>
                </button>
                <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-slate-500 uppercase">MP4 // ~3.5 min</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Daily Drop */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 space-y-0.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-400">
                <Disc className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-100">⚡ Daily Drop</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                Press once every 24 hours to mint today&apos;s album.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Cargo Bay & Director's Chair */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 space-y-0.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-400">
                <Layers className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-100">The Cargo Bay &amp; Director&apos;s Chair</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                Click the gear icon to manage modular expansion packs for vocabulary, subjects, and backdrops—or disable default assets entirely to isolate custom packs.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Custom Cover Photos */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 space-y-0.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sky-400">
                <Sliders className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-100">Custom Cover Photos</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                Toggle custom photos to run your own snapshots through the 1970s Kodachrome wear and halftone aging engine.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Temporal Pressings */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 space-y-0.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-cyan-400">
                <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-100">Temporal Pressings</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                Any record pressed in a year beyond your device&apos;s present calendar triggers an iridescent lenticular holographic security sash from the future.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* The Crate Archive */}
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 border border-slate-800/80 space-y-0.5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-emerald-400">
                <Archive className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                <span className="font-bold text-xs text-slate-100">The Crate Archive</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10.5px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                Tap <strong className="text-slate-200">Archive</strong> in the top header to browse your back-catalog, favorite standout pressings, or export sleeve cards.
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Collapsible: Troubleshooting & Manual Reset */}
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-900/90 border border-slate-800 overflow-hidden transition-all">
              <div
                onClick={() => setIsTroubleshootingCollapsed(!isTroubleshootingCollapsed)}
                className="p-2.5 flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-800/60 select-none transition-colors"
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                  {isTroubleshootingCollapsed ? (
                    <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 shrink-0" />
                  ) : (
                    <ChevronDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-300 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-amber-400 min-w-0">
                    <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="font-bold text-xs tracking-wide uppercase truncate">
                      Troubleshooting &amp; Manual Reset
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {!isTroubleshootingCollapsed && (
                <div className="p-2.5 pt-1.5 space-y-2.5 border-t border-slate-800/60 bg-black/20 animate-in fade-in duration-150">
                  {/* Actionable Reset Trigger */}
                  <div className="p-2 rounded-lg bg-amber-950/30 border border-amber-800/40 space-y-1.5">
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-300 block uppercase">
                      One-Tap Cache &amp; Storage Purge:
                    </span>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                      Instantly unregisters service workers, clears cache storage, and reloads fresh application assets.
                    </p>
                    {!confirmPurge ? (
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setConfirmPurge(true)}
                        className="w-full py-1.5 px-2.5 rounded-md bg-amber-500/20 hover:bg-amber-500/30 border border-amber-500/50 text-amber-200 hover:text-white font-mono font-bold text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95"
                      >
                        <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-400" />
                        <span>Purge Cache &amp; Reset Deck</span>
                      </button>
                    ) : (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 pt-0.5">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setConfirmPurge(false)}
                          className="flex-1 py-1 px-2 rounded bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-mono text-[10px]"
                        >
                          Cancel
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => performFactoryReset()}
                          className="flex-1 py-1 px-2 rounded bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-mono font-bold text-[10px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1"
                        >
                          <Check className="w-3 h-3" />
                          <span>Confirm Reset</span>
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 leading-tight">
                    Or clear browser storage manually if the app remains stuck:
                  </p>
                  <div className="space-y-1 text-[9.5px] text-slate-400 font-mono leading-relaxed bg-black/40 p-2 rounded border border-slate-800/80">
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-200">iOS Safari:</strong> Settings → Safari → Advanced → Website Data → Delete.
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-200">Android Chrome:</strong> Settings → Apps → Chrome → Storage → Clear Storage.
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <strong className="text-slate-200">Desktop:</strong> Press <strong className="text-slate-200">Ctrl/Cmd + Shift + R</strong>.
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Footer */}
          <div className="px-4 py-2.5 border-t border-slate-800 bg-[#0c101d] flex items-center justify-between shrink-0">
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-500">INFO DOC v1.0</span>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-400 text-slate-950 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm active:scale-95 cursor-pointer"
            >
              <span>Understood</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* In-App Walkthrough Video Player Overlay */}
      {isPlayingGuideVideo && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[80] flex flex-col bg-black animate-in fade-in select-none">
          <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 bg-black/90 border-b border-amber-900/50 shrink-0 z-10 pt-[calc(0.75rem+env(safe-area-inset-top))]">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-400 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold tracking-wider uppercase text-amber-200">
                Walkthrough // Functionality Guide
              </span>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsPlayingGuideVideo(false)}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 border border-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white hover:bg-slate-800 transition-colors text-xs font-mono flex items-center gap-1.5 active:scale-95 cursor-pointer"
              aria-label="Close walkthrough"
            >
              <span>Close</span>
              <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="flex-1 w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center bg-black overflow-hidden p-2 pb-[calc(1rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))]">
            <video
              src={`${(import.meta.env.BASE_URL || '/').replace(/\/$/, '')}/video/guide.mp4`}
              controls
              autoPlay
              playsInline
              onEnded={() => setIsPlayingGuideVideo(false)}
              className="max-w-full max-h-full rounded-lg object-contain shadow-2xl"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </>
  );
};
</file>

interface TopBarProps {

  onToggleFavorite: () => void;

  isStandalone?: boolean;

export const TopBar: React.FC<TopBarProps> = ({
  onOpenArchive,
  isToday,
  onGoToToday,
  isFavorite,
  onToggleFavorite,
  onShare,
  onOpenApparel,
  hasEntries,
  hasAtjBadge,

  return (

        <span className="tracking-wide hidden sm:inline">Archive</span>
      </button>

              : 'bg-amber-400 shadow-[0_0_6px_#f59e0b] border-amber-200/40 animate-pulse'

          <span className="font-mono font-black text-[11px] sm:text-xs tracking-[0.08em] sm:tracking-[0.18em] text-[#f3ede4] uppercase drop-shadow">
            Y.D.R.B.N.
          </span>

          {/* DYNAMIC TRANSFORM: SN: CRK3 morphs into lit ATJ trigger badge */}
          {hasAtjBadge ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onOpenAtjVideo}
              aria-label="Play Archival Transmission"
              title="Archival Vault // ATJ-01 (Tap to play)"
              className="cursor-pointer text-[8px] font-mono font-bold tracking-widest text-emerald-300 uppercase px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-emerald-950/90 border border-emerald-500/60 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(16,185,129,0.4)] hover:bg-emerald-900 active:scale-90 transition-all animate-in fade-in"
            >
              ATJ
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className="text-[7.5px] sm:text-[8px] font-mono font-bold tracking-wider sm:tracking-widest text-amber-400/90 uppercase px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-black/50 border border-amber-700/30">
              SN: CRK3
            </span>
          )}

        {/* Apparel / Merch Button */}
        {onOpenApparel && (
          <button
            type="button"

            disabled={!hasEntries}
            aria-label="Apparel Fabrication"

            className="h-8 w-7 sm:w-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-slate-800/60 text-amber-400/90 border border-slate-700 hover:text-amber-300 hover:border-amber-500/40 transition-all active:scale-90 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:cursor-not-allowed cursor-pointer shrink-0"
          >
